*Shohei Kiryu,Kazufumi Tanji,Yoshihiro Ueda,Kosuke Fukui,Masahiro Takeoka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在光学量子传感中，使用预示概率性非高斯态是否优于确定性高斯态的问题，提出有效量子费舍信息（EQFI）作为评估指标，证明其不超过原始高斯态的量子费舍信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯态在量子传感中具有高量子费舍信息，但其生成通常是概率性的，限制了实际性能；需评估考虑生成成功率后的资源利用效率。

Method: 利用无源线性光学系统中光子数守恒的性质，将参数编码前的预示态制备映射为编码后的后选择问题，并引入成功概率加权的有效量子费舍信息（EQFI）。

Result: 证明了EQFI不会超过原始高斯输入态的QFI，表明在考虑生成概率后，预示性非高斯态并不优于原始高斯态。

Conclusion: 在单参数相位估计中，考虑成功概率后，预示性非高斯态的计量性能不优于原始高斯态，强调了在量子传感中合理计算资源的重要性。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian states can exhibit large quantum Fisher information (QFI) in quantum sensing. In optical systems, however, its generation is often probabilistic via the boson-sampling type conditional operation and thus its generation rate is limited. This probabilistic generation of non-Gaussian resource should be taken into account for evaluation of the sensing performance. Then a natural question arising is whether the use of heralded probabilistic non-Gaussian states is better than that of the original deterministic Gaussian states for quantum sensing. In this paper, we answer to this question for single-parameter phase-estimation. By using photon-number conservation in passive linear optical systems, we show that heralded state preparation before parameter encoding can be mapped to a postselection problem after parameter encoding for phase estimation. This mapping allows the success probability of heralding to be included naturally in the metrological performance. We introduce an effective quantum Fisher information (EQFI), defined as the success-probability-weighted QFI of the heralded outputs, and prove that it cannot exceed the QFI of the original Gaussian inputs. The result highlights the importance of resource counting in quantum sensing toward better understanding of the resource efficient advantage of optical quantum sensing.

</details>


### [88] [Exponential logical-error reduction in quantum memories via optimal syndrome-measurement timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06242)
*Tobias Haug,Kishor Bharti,Leandro Aolita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对量子存储器的可优化综合征测量时间间隔模型，通过分析和模拟表明，最优测量间隔与码距成反比，能显著降低逻辑错误率，并提出基于综合征活动的自适应策略以应对时变噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子存储中，测量时间间隔过长会导致空闲错误累积，过短则引入测量诱发错误，因此需要优化测量时序以平衡噪声影响。

Method: 建立了一个描述逻辑噪声与测量间隔关系的经验模型，理论分析最优间隔与码距的关系，并提出基于实际综合征活动的自适应测量策略，通过旋转表面码仿真和匹配解码进行验证。

Result: 理论证明最优测量间隔与码距成反比，可实现逻辑错误率的指数级抑制；自适应策略在时变噪声下优于固定间隔方案，尤其在强噪声爆发时表现更优；模拟结果支持该模型，且基于谷歌实验参数预测单位时间逻辑错误率最高可降低40%。

Conclusion: 综合征测量的时间间隔是可优化的关键参数，采用距离依赖的最优间隔和自适应策略可显著提升量子存储器的纠错性能。

Abstract: Syndrome-measurements timing is usually treated as a fixed clock cycle of a quantum error-correcting code. For quantum memories, however, the intra-measurement interval is itself an optimizable control parameter: measuring too rarely allows idling errors to accumulate, whereas measuring too often introduces measurement-induced faults. We propose a phenomenological logical-noise model for this trade-off and analytically show that the optimal syndrome-measurements interval scales inversely proportionally with the code distance and that this produces an exponential reduction of logical-error rates in the distance relative to constant-interval schedules. Furthermore, for time-dependent idling noise, we develop an adaptive timing strategy based on the measured syndrome activity that outperforms every fixed-interval protocol, with largest gains for short but strong noise bursts. Simulations of rotated surface-code memories with matching decoding validate the phenomenological model, the distance-dependent optimum, and the adaptive-strategy improvement. Moreover, with the experimental noise parameters reported by Google in Nature 638 (2025), our model predicts reductions in logical-error rates per unit time of up to $40\%$.

</details>


### [89] [Nonlinear Compton scattering in a quantized pump field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06289)
*Kenan Qu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种由单模量子场驱动的非线性康普顿散射的全量子化理论，考虑了泵浦耗尽、反作用和末态关联效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一个精确的量子理论来描述在强场量子电动力学中非线性康普顿散射的完整量子特性，包括场的量子涨落和光子数相关效应。

Method: 利用精确的量子-沃科夫态，结合置换或压缩-置换福克态阶梯，处理有限福克态泵浦下的散射过程，并在明亮弱耗尽极限下使用维格纳函数加权平均方法进行简化分析。

Result: 发现有限福克态泵浦导致离散的光子转移边和终端谱截止；对于压缩相干光，压缩角通过光子数涨落控制高能辐射特性；谐波结构由普通和广义贝塞尔函数分别描述圆极化和线极化情况。

Conclusion: 该理论统一描述了泵浦场量子效应在非线性康普顿散射中的作用，为强场QED中光-物质相互作用的全量子模拟提供了基础。

Abstract: We develop a fully quantized theory of nonlinear Compton scattering driven by a single-mode quantum field. Exact quantum-Volkov states retain pump depletion, back-action, and final-state correlations through displaced or squeezed-displaced Fock-state ladders. A finite Fock-state pump produces discrete photon-transfer edges and a terminal spectral cutoff. In the bright, weakly depleted regime, the exact theory reduces to a Wigner-function weighted-average of scattering probabilities evaluated at fixed complex field amplitudes, with ordinary and generalized Bessel functions describing the harmonic structure for circular and linear polarization, respectively. For squeezed coherent light, the squeezing angle controls the high-energy emission through photon-number fluctuations.

</details>


### [90] [Impact of Nonlinearities on Local Kinetic and Thermokinetic Uncertainty Relations in Bosonic Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06303)
*Didrik Palmqvist,Luca Magazzù,Milena Grifoni,Janine Splettstoesser*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究非线性效应对局部动力学和热动力学精度界限的影响，并在两个实验相关模型中验证其可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非线性或相互作用如何影响局部不确定性关系的有效性和预测能力，以及这种影响是否依赖于活性的具体定义。

Method: 通过分析谐振子网络和非线性的自旋-玻色子模型这两个实验相关的系统，研究非线性对局部动能和热动能精度界限的影响。

Result: 发现在强耦合下，非线性可能破坏局部动力学和热动力学的精度界限，且影响程度取决于活性的定义。

Conclusion: 非线性显著影响局部不确定性关系的预测能力，研究结果提供了可实验检验的预测。

Abstract: The precision of transport observables can be bounded by the entropy production and the activity of the transport process via kinetic and thermokinetic uncertainty relations. In linear bosonic systems, such uncertainty relations can provide tight bounds when the activity is replaced by a local activity of the measurement contact of interest---even in the strong-coupling regime. How much nonlinearities (or interactions) impact the validity and predictiveness of these local bounds and how much this impact depends on the concrete definition of activity are open questions that we address for two experimentally relevant model systems, a harmonic oscillator network and the intrinsically nonlinear spin-boson model. We thereby provide experimentally testable predictions on how nonlinearities impact or even break local kinetic and thermokinetic precision bounds.

</details>


### [91] [Time-Reversal Selection Rules for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06304)
*Eric Kubischta,Ian Teixeira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过应用时间反演对称性到量子编码，本文揭示了物理误差代数上的奇偶选择规则，并表明单量子比特错误检测可隐含纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索时间反演对称性在量子纠错码中的作用，以简化错误检测与纠正条件。

Method: 利用时间反演对称性分析量子码的代数结构，结合Knill-Laflamme条件和Rains shadow enumerator进行推导。

Result: 发现奇数个自旋上的时间反演不变逻辑量子比特为Kramers双重态，导致所有偶权重Pauli算子表现为标量，从而使所有偶权重Knill-Laflamme条件自动满足。

Conclusion: 时间反演对称性为量子纠错提供了新的结构约束，使得单量子比特错误检测足以实现纠正。

Abstract: We apply time-reversal symmetry to quantum codes and show that it imposes parity selection rules on the physical error algebra. A time-reversal-invariant logical qubit on an odd number of spins is a Kramers doublet, forcing every even-weight Pauli to act as a scalar. Consequently, all even-weight Knill--Laflamme conditions hold automatically, so single-qubit error detection implies correction. We then reinterpret the Rains shadow enumerator through time reversal: each coefficient is a sum of error-resolved overlaps between a code and its time-reversed image.

</details>


### [92] [On Optimal Quantum Data Hiding and Maximal Separable Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06308)
*Zhi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子数据隐藏问题，针对不同类型的受限测量，建立了精确结果和改进的界限。证明了在分离测量和LOCC测量下的最优数据隐藏比率均为min{n,m}，并提供了有限轮LOCC的具体实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在全局测量被限制为局部测量和经典通信时，区分能力可能损失的程度。

Method: 通过分析Schatten p-球的最大中心化半径，以及使用高斯秩一POVM等方法，推导出不同受限测量类别的最优数据隐藏比率。

Result: 证明了对于C^n⊗C^m上的双部分系统，分离测量和LOCC测量的最优数据隐藏比率均为min{n,m}；对于Alice先进行的一方LOCC，最优比率为(1+o(1))n；对于无通信的局部操作，通用上界改进为(π√3/4+o(1))min{n,m}。

Conclusion: 本文给出了多种受限测量下量子数据隐藏的最优比率，并增强了经典分离球定理的结果，同时提供了有限轮LOCC的显式实现。

Abstract: Quantum data hiding asks how much distinguishing power can be lost when global measurements are restricted to local measurements and classical communication. In this work, we establish sharp results and improved bounds for several natural classes of restricted measurements. For bipartite systems on $\mathbb C^n\otimes\mathbb C^m$, we prove that the optimal data-hiding ratios against separable and LOCC measurements are both $\min\{n,m\}$. This result follows from a stronger result that, for every $2\le p\le\infty$, the largest centered Schatten $p$-ball whose associated binary measurements are implementable by finite-round LOCC has radius $\min\{n,m\}^{2/p-1}$. This strengthens the classic separable-ball theorems, while also providing an explicit finite-round LOCC implementation. For Alice-first one-way LOCC with Alice's local dimension equal to $n$, we prove that the optimal ratio is $(1+o(1))n$, with the upper bound obtained from a Gaussian rank-one POVM. For local operations without communication, we improve the universal upper bound to $(π\sqrt3/4+o(1))\min\{n,m\}$.

</details>


### [93] [Dimension-Free Polylogarithmic Quantum Shadow Tomography from Sequential Pretty-Good Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06345)
*Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Qizhao Huang,Lenny Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的量子协议，用于解决影子层析成像问题，其样本复杂度在可观测量数量上具有多对数依赖性，并且与未知量子态的维度无关，从而回答了Aaronson提出的开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Aaronson提出的关于影子层析成像是否具有维度无关且仅对可观测量数量有多对数依赖的样本复杂度的开放性问题。

Method: 通过极小化极大论证将一般影子层析问题转化为有限集合估计问题，然后开发了一种与可观测量无关的协议，反复应用“相当好”的测量并根据测量结果更新先验分布。

Result: 得到了样本复杂度为O((log(m/δ))^4 / (log log(m/δ))^3)的结果，这是在可观测量数量上的多对数依赖，并且独立于量子态的维度。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅回答了Aaronson的原始问题，还相比之前最佳结果实现了指数级改进。

Abstract: \textit{Shadow tomography} is a fundamental problem in quantum information theory. Given multiple copies of an unknown $d$-dimensional quantum state $ρ$ and a known collection of observables ${E_1,\ldots,E_m}$, the goal is to estimate all expectation values $\{\Tr(ρE_i)\}_{i=1}^m$ to additive accuracy $\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$.
  An elusive open question from the seminal shadow tomography work of Aaronson (STOC'18) is whether this task admits a dimension-independent sample complexity with only polylogarithmic dependence on $m$, as suggested by the best-known lower bounds. In this work, we give a quantum protocol for shadow tomography with sample complexity \[ O\left( \frac{1}{\varepsilon^2} \frac{(\log (m/δ))^4} {(\log\log (m/δ))^3} \right), \] which is polylogarithmic in the number of observables and independent of the dimension of the unknown state thereby answering Aaronson's original question while also providing an exponential improvement in the prior best dimension independent sample complexity of shadow tomography from Sinha (STOC'25).
  Our approach first reduces the general shadow-tomography problem to a finite-ensemble estimation problem via a minimax argument. We then develop an observable-independent protocol that repeatedly applies the pretty-good measurement and updates the priori distribution over the finite ensemble according to the measurement outcomes. A refined tail analysis of the resulting estimation error yields simultaneous accuracy guarantees for all observables.

</details>


### [94] [Bell nonlocality from twisted statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06359)
*Ivana Đorđ ević,Jovan Potrebić,Aleksandra Gočanin,Dragoljub Gočanin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了非对易Moyal平面上自由量子标量场的贝尔关联，通过扭曲多粒子统计揭示非对易结构对接收实验室间纠缠的影响，并表明局域测量可违背CHSH不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非对易空间中量子场论的多粒子效应及其对贝尔非定域性的影响。

Method: 利用扭缠Fock空间表示和扭曲多粒子统计，结合经典外部源激发相干动量对态，分析双局域模式测量下的CHSH不等式违背。

Result: 发现动量依赖的扭缠相位导致不可分解的模态纠缠，合适的局域测量可导致CHSH不等式违背。

Conclusion: 非对易结构通过多粒子扇区影响量子关联，贝尔测量可作为探测非对易性的操作手段。

Abstract: We investigate Bell correlations for a free real quantum scalar field on the noncommutative Moyal plane. Although the free field dynamics and the one-particle sector remain unchanged, the deformation enters through twisted multiparticle statistics and its Fock-space dressing representation. A classical external source coupled locally to the twist-dressed quantum field prepares coherent superpositions of momentum-pair configurations propagating toward two spacelike-separated laboratories. The momentum-dependent twist phases are generally nonfactorizable and generate entanglement between the corresponding wave-packet modes. We show that suitable local mode measurements lead to a violation of the CHSH Bell inequality. The resulting correlations provide an operational probe of the noncommutative structure encoded in the multiparticle sector of the quantum field.

</details>
