### [95] [TSDM: A Scheduling Policy for Joint Throughput-AoI Optimization in Multichannel Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05348)
*Lin Wang,I-Hong Hou*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为TSDM的两阶段调度框架，用于在多信道无线网络中联合优化信息年龄（AoI）和吞吐量，理论证明其有效性并在仿真中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有异构和不可靠信道的系统中，联合优化低信息年龄（AoI）和高吞吐量是一个分析上具有挑战性的问题，尤其是在远程传感应用中对这两者均有高要求。

Method: 提出TSDM：一个两阶段缺陷匹配调度框架。第一阶段将效用最大化目标转化为每个节点-信道对传输的目标均值和时间方差统计量；第二阶段采用低复杂度的加权匹配缺陷（WMD）规则进行实时信道分配。该方法基于二阶统计特性（均值和时间方差）来表征每个数据流的性能。

Result: 理论上证明了TSDM能够实现每个数据流所期望的均值和时间方差；在两个公开的联合吞吐量-AoI优化问题上的大量仿真表明，TSDM显著优于现有的调度策略。

Conclusion: TSDM通过二阶统计建模和两阶段设计，有效解决了多信道无线网络中AoI与吞吐量的联合优化难题，兼具理论保证和优异的实证性能。

Abstract: Optimizing for both low Age of Information (AoI) and high throughput is critical for remote sensing applications that rely on multichannel wireless networks. However, jointly optimizing these two metrics is an analytically challenging problem, particularly in systems with heterogeneous and unreliable channels. To address this challenge, we propose TSDM, a Two-Stage Deficit Matching scheduling framework. TSDM is based on a second-order approach that characterizes the performance of each data flow by its mean and temporal variance. In the first stage, TSDM translates the high-level utility maximization objective into a concrete set of target mean and temporal variance statistics for transmissions over each node-channel pair. In the second stage, a low-complexity Weighted Matching Deficit (WMD) rule performs real-time channel assignment. We theoretically prove that TSDM achieves the desired mean and temporal variance for each flow. Furthermore, we conduct extensive simulations on two open joint throughput-AoI optimization problems. In both cases, TSDM significantly outperforms existing scheduling policies.

</details>


### [96] [Katz Centrality-Based Security Allocation in Positive Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05360)
*Anh Tung Nguyen,Sribalaji C. Anand,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了在隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击下，基于正权有向图的网络化控制系统中的安全分配问题，提出了通过半定规划和Katz中心性度量来优化监控节点选择的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对网络化控制系统中隐蔽攻击导致的性能损失问题，如何有效分配监控资源以抑制攻击影响成为关键挑战。

Method: 通过建立半定规划（SDP）模型上界分析最坏情况下的网络性能损失，并结合Katz中心性度量构建与网络规模无关的优化方法，提出基于Katz中心性的启发式搜索算法选择次优监控节点。

Result: 证明了最坏情况下的网络性能损失可由一个可处理的半定规划问题上界约束；建立了该SDP问题与Katz中心性之间的理论联系；提出了无需求解优化问题的启发式监控节点选择方法，并通过不同规模的Erdos-Renyi随机图仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法为大规模网络化控制系统提供了实用的安全防护策略，能够在不依赖复杂优化计算的情况下有效应对多种隐蔽攻击场景。

Abstract: This paper deals with security allocation challenges for networked control systems represented by positive-weighted digraphs under stealthy false data injection attacks. These systems consist of interconnected subsystems, referred to as nodes in the underlying digraph, where an adversary aims to maximize network performance loss by stealthily attacking specific nodes. Meanwhile, a defender monitors several nodes to impose stealthiness constraints on the adversary's actions, thereby minimizing the network performance loss. We analyze the worst-case network performance loss of these stealthy attacks and make the following contributions: we (i) show that the worst-case network performance loss is upper-bounded by a tractable semi-definite programming (SDP) problem; (ii) establish the relationship between the SDP problem and the Katz centrality measure of the underlying digraph under a sufficient condition, resulting in a network-size-independent optimization problem; and (iii) provide a heuristic search based on the Katz centrality measure of the underlying digraph for selecting sub-optimal monitor nodes against all admissible attack scenarios without solving optimization problems. These results offer practical insights for safeguarding large-scale networked control systems against stealthy false data injection attacks. The obtained results are validated via extensive simulations on Erdos-Renyi random graphs with different network sizes.

</details>


### [97] [A Passivity-Based Analysis of First-Order Momentum-Based Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05492)
*Sepehr Moalemi,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文通过基于无源性的分析方法，研究了一类具有梯度上下界的函数的动量优化算法，利用环路变换将动量法表示为与输出严格无源系统负反馈连接的无源控制器，并基于弱无源性定理推导出超参数条件，保证梯度渐近消失，在附加条件下证明了迭代序列收敛到全局最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解动量方法在一类满足梯度扇形条件的函数上的收敛行为，本文引入离散时间无源系统理论进行分析，以建立更清晰的稳定性与收敛性条件。

Method: 采用离散时间无源性理论，通过对动量算法进行环路变换，将其建模为无源控制器与输出严格无源（OSP）系统的负反馈连接，应用弱无源性定理推导收敛所需的显式超参数条件。

Result: 得到了确保梯度渐近消失的明确超参数条件，并在额外假设下证明了迭代序列收敛到全局最小点。

Conclusion: 动量方法可以通过无源控制框架进行有效分析，该视角为优化算法的设计和稳定性提供了新的理论工具和见解。

Abstract: This paper presents a discrete-time passivity-based analysis of first-order momentum-based methods for a class of functions whose gradient has lower and upper sector bounds of $0$ and $L$, respectively. Through a loop transformation, it is shown that momentum-based methods can be represented as a passive controller in negative feedback with an output strictly passive (OSP) system. The weak passivity theorem is then used to derive explicit hyperparameter conditions under which the shifted gradient asymptotically vanishes. Under an additional assumption that requires the existence of a unique stationary point and excludes arbitrarily small gradients far from that point, convergence of the iterates to the global minimizer is established.

</details>


### [98] [Multilayer Dual-polarized Microstrip Antenna Design by Topology Optimization with Enhanced Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05712)
*Pan Lu,Eddie Wadbro,Viktor Lundström,Jonas Starck,Martin Berggren,Emadeldeen Hassan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于密度拓扑优化的方法来设计具有增强带宽的双极化微带天线，通过优化FR4叠层中的铜层，在5.7 GHz附近实现了高隔离度（>40 dB）和约10%的阻抗带宽，并通过实验验证了仿真与测量结果的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双极化微带天线带宽有限，限制了其在高效无线通信系统中的应用，因此需要一种能够同时提升带宽、端口匹配和隔离性能的设计方法。

Method: 采用基于密度的拓扑优化方法，同时优化两层FR4基板上的铜结构，构建了一个综合考虑馈电端口匹配、端口隔离和远场双极化性能的优化问题。

Result: 设计了两个工作在5.7 GHz附近的双极化天线，实现了超过40 dB的端口隔离和约10%的阻抗带宽，仿真与实测结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的拓扑优化方法能有效提升双极化微带天线的带宽和整体性能，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Dual-polarized (DP) microstrip antennas are utilized in wireless systems for efficient data transmission. However, their bandwidth is typically very limited. In this contribution, we propose to design DP microstrip antennas with enhanced bandwidth using a density-based topology optimization approach. We formulate an optimization problem that simultaneously accounts for feeding port matching, the ports' isolation, and far-field dual-polarized performance. To enhance the bandwidth, we employ an FR4 stack-up, in which the copper on two layers is optimized simultaneously. We present two antenna designs operating around 5.7 GHz, which show a compromise in performance between a high isolation (more than 40 dB) and enhanced impedance bandwidth (around 10%). The optimized designs are experimentally validated, showing an excellent agreement between the simulated and measured performance.

</details>


### [99] [Moisture Effects in Medium-Voltage Underground Distribution Systems: a perspective overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06074)
*Diana Enescu,Elena Otilia Virjoghe,Simone Vincenzo Suraci,Luigi Calcara,Radu Porumb,Lucia Rosso,Giulio Beltramino,Valentina Picco,Andrea Mazza*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文综述了湿气对中压地下电缆系统及其附件的影响，强调通过改进设计、安装、监测和维护中的湿气管理，可提高配电系统的可靠性，支持能源转型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于配电系统在能源转型中的关键作用，湿气侵入可能引发多种故障，影响系统可靠性，因此需要全面理解湿气对不同类型绝缘材料的影响机制。

Method: 综合文献研究与配电系统运营商的实践经验，并结合多个科研项目成果，分析湿气在化学、物理、机械、热力和电气等方面的作用机理。

Result: 发现绝缘材料的选择对缓解湿气侵入的多重后果至关重要，且不同传统与新型材料对湿气的响应各异；同时评估了用于检测外部和内部湿气的诊断技术。

Conclusion: 通过在整个生命周期中优化湿气管理，可提升中压配电系统的可靠性，减少停电，从而支持更稳健的能源基础设施建设。

Abstract: The distribution system is becoming a fundamental part for enabling the energy transition. The fault causes may be several and of different nature, embracing a variety of specializations. This review aims at providing an integrated overview of moisture effects across underground Medium Voltage (MV) cable systems and their accessories. The study collects both literature contributions and practical experiences derived from distribution system operators and research projects on the topic. The findings highlight that the choice of the insulation material is fundamental for mitigating the various consequences of the moisture ingress, linked to chemical and physical processes, as well as mechanical, thermal, and electrical factors, all of which are analysed in detail in the paper. The paper examines the effects of moisture on different types of materials, both traditional and innovative, and discusses diagnostic techniques to detect moisture ingress from the surrounding environment as well as internally generated moisture. The path towards increasing MV distribution reliability, reducing outages, and support the energy transition with more robust distribution infrastructure, is hence achievable through improving moisture management across design, installation, monitoring, and maintenance.

</details>


### [100] [Distributed coordination for transmission-distribution systems with nonlinear flexibility aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06341)
*Xinliang Dai,Yanlin Jiang,Frederik Zahn,Yi Guo,Veit Hagenmeyer*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非迭代的预测-校正聚合方法，用于输电-配电协调，利用路径跟踪技术实现可扩展计算并保证误差界限，显著降低了误判灵活性和丢失灵活性的概率，并大幅减少了端到端计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高比例分布式能源资源（DERs）使配电网在输配一体化运行中扮演更积极的角色，但现有线性模型可能误判交流可行操作点，而非线性聚合又计算成本高昂，尤其在多时段协调中。因此需要一种高效且精确的聚合方法。

Method: 将输电-配电协调问题重构为分层优化框架，引入基于实时最优控制中路径跟踪技术的非迭代预测-校正聚合方法，实现可追踪计算并提供误差保证。

Result: 在24个辐射状配电网案例和7个环网变体（包括真实的KIT Campus North电网）上测试，该方法相比线性代理模型和凸松弛方法显著降低了采样的误判和丢失灵活性率；在两个24时段的输配协同测试案例中，端到端实际计算时间比集中式方法减少6倍，主要得益于降维效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的预测-校正聚合方法在保持计算高效的同时提高了灵活性聚合的准确性，适用于大规模、多时段的输配协同优化，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: High shares of distributed energy resources (DERs) transform distribution systems into active participants in integrated transmission and distribution (ITD) operations. Linear models enable scalable distribution-level flexibility aggregation but can misclassify AC feasible operating points, whereas direct nonlinear aggregation becomes costly, especially in multiperiod ITD coordination. This paper reformulates transmission-distribution coordination within a hierarchical optimization framework and introduces a non-iterative predictor-corrector aggregation method. By leveraging path-following techniques from real-time optimal control, the approach achieves tractable computation with guaranteed error bounds. Across 24 radial distribution-network cases and seven meshed variants, including the real KIT Campus North grid, the proposed method yields substantially lower sampled false- and lost-flexibility rates than linear surrogates and a convex relaxation. On two 24-period ITD testcases, the formulation reduces end-to-end wall-clock time by factors of 6 relative to the corresponding centralized formulation, primarily through dimensionality reduction.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Cross-Sectional Heterogeneity in LSTM Networks for Financial Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05755)
*Julius Döbelt*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合宏观金融变量和可学习行业嵌入的改进型LSTM模型，用于提升股票收益预测的准确性与可解释性，在S&P 500成分股上的实证结果显示其优于多种基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于市场效率和低信噪比，资产收益预测极具挑战；传统深度学习模型难以捕捉资产间的横截面异质性，尤其是行业层面的差异。

Method: 提出一种改进的LSTM架构，引入宏观金融协变量和可学习的行业嵌入以建模跨行业异质性，并通过日度多空交易策略进行评估，使用潜空间可视化和权重分析提升模型可解释性。

Result: 该模型在风险调整收益等指标上优于基础LSTM、随机森林和买入持有策略，行业嵌入有效提升了对行业动态的适应能力，且发现短期反转和行业动量是主要预测因子。

Conclusion: 融合结构化先验（如行业嵌入）可增强深度学习模型在金融时序预测中的性能与可解释性，为解决金融数据中的横截面异质性提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Predicting financial asset returns remains one of the most difficult challenges in empirical finance, driven by the low signal-to-noise ratio and the semi-strong form of market efficiency. While deep learning models, especially LSTM networks, have shown promise in capturing temporal dependencies, standard architectures often struggle to account for the cross-sectional heterogeneity of asset returns. This paper proposes a novel architectural extension to the basic LSTM model designed to improve both predictive accuracy and model interpretability. The framework integrates macro-financial covariates to capture broader economic signals and learnable sector embeddings to encompass heterogeneity by sector. The trading strategy involves constructing a long-short portfolio based on daily directional forecasts for each S&P 500 constituent, targeting stocks expected to under- or outperform the cross-sectional median return of the S&P 500. Model Performance is evaluated against three competitive benchmarks: a basic LSTM, a Random Forest model and a traditional market buy-and-hold strategy. The empirical results demonstrate that the LSTM with sector embeddings outperforms all benchmarks across key risk and return metrics. By utilizing sector embeddings, the model explicitly incorporates cross-sectional heterogeneity, allowing it to adapt to varying industry dynamics within the market. To address the black-box nature of deep learning, I use latent space visualizations to analyse how the model differentiates between sectors, providing insights into the internal representation of the sectors in the LSTM. The impact of the sector information can be quantified using a novel contribution metric by inspecting the weights of the LSTM. The predictive signal is driven by a short-term reversal factor and an industry momentum factor.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [The Structure of Spreading on Temporal Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06175)
*Omar Henderson,Mikko Kivelä,Márton Karsai*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种将时序网络中的传播动力学映射到时序事件图可达性分析的理论框架，可用于计算流行病传播率和阈值，避免显式模拟，具有显著计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解静态网络中传播过程已有成熟方法（如渗流理论），但时序网络缺乏类似的有效分析工具，因此需要建立新的理论框架以高效研究动态传播过程。

Method: 将时序网络中的传播动力学映射到时序事件图的可达性分析，利用事件图的连通分量特性推导流行病的流行率和传播阈值，无需进行显式仿真。

Result: 得到了适用于任意度分布和事件间隔时间分布的时序网络的流行病流行率和阈值的解析结果，计算效率显著高于传统模拟方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为时序网络上的传播过程提供了类似于静态网络中渗流理论的分析工具，极大提升了对动态传播现象的理论理解和计算效率。

Abstract: The physics of spreading in static networks is well understood through mappings to percolation. We show that spreading dynamics on temporal networks can analogously be mapped to reachability in temporal event graphs. This provides a theoretical and computational framework for a class of processes, such as variants of the susceptible-infected-susceptible model. Without explicit simulations, through the component analysis of event graphs, we obtain epidemic prevalence and derive epidemic thresholds for temporal networks with arbitrary degree and inter-event time distributions, with significant computational advantages as compared to explicit simulations.

</details>


### [103] [Interaction size and dissent tolerance in majority-rule dynamics with collective reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05789)
*Roni Muslim,Rinto Anugraha NQZ,Qonuni Gusthaf Haq,Fahrudin Nugroho,Idham Syah Alam,Elida Lailiya Istiqomah*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多数规则模型，通过引入异议容忍度$d$，使得即使在高度一致的群体中，少数成员的异议也能触发集体反转。该机制改变了系统的相变结构，并恢复了大交互规模下的相变能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在群体决策过程中，少数异议如何影响集体行为的反转，尤其是在接近 unanimity（全体一致）的情况下是否仍能实现状态转变。传统严格一致模型存在局限性，因此需要扩展以解释更广泛的现实场景。

Method: 采用平均场分析与数值模拟相结合的方法，研究不同交互规模$n$和异议容忍度$d$对系统动力学的影响，探讨相变边界及吸引子结构的变化。

Result: 发现允许异议显著扩展了发生相变的参数范围，打破了仅在n=3和n=4时才存在物理可及相变的限制；在足够宽的激活窗口下，方向不对称性可通过鞍结分岔消除一个有序吸引子，导致单一稳定态；增加异议容忍度可缩短达成共识的瞬态时间，但不改变其对数尺度依赖性。

Conclusion: 异议容忍作为独立控制参数，能够调节集体排序、双稳态以及共识动力学，为理解复杂系统中的集体行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce a majority-rule model in which collective reversal can be activated in highly aligned groups even when a limited number of members dissent. The dissent tolerance $d$ extends the strict-unanimity dynamics by making near-unanimous group compositions eligible for reversal. Mean-field analysis and simulations reveal that this change qualitatively alters the phase structure. Under strict unanimity, a physically accessible transition exists only for $n=3$ and $n=4$. Allowing dissent restores transitions at larger interaction sizes, replacing the fixed interaction-size threshold with an accessibility boundary in the $(n,d)$ plane. When the activation window is sufficiently broad, directional asymmetry can eliminate one of the two ordered attractors through a saddle-node bifurcation, producing a single stable collective state. In the one-sided case, increasing the dissent tolerance can shorten the transient approach to consensus but leaves its leading logarithmic dependence on population size unchanged. Activation selectivity acts as an independent control parameter for collective ordering, bistability, and consensus dynamics.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [A variational framework for Bayesian inversion in moving boundary Darcy flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05317)
*Michael E. Causon,Marco A. Iglesias,Michael V. Tretyakov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种无限维贝叶斯框架，用于通过压力观测反演单相达西流中随空间变化的对数渗透率，结合移动边界问题，利用代表元方法实现高效的最大后验估计与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受树脂传递模塑工艺启发，旨在通过压力数据有效反演具有移动边界的渗流系统中的空间变对数渗透率场，并实现高效且稳健的参数估计与不确定性量化。

Method: 构建基于高斯先验Cameron-Martin空间的无限维贝叶斯框架，引入观测相关的代表元公式，导出约化Levenberg-Marquardt算法用于求解MAP估计，并给出线性化MAP近似的显式低秩协方差；在1D中使用显式解分析后验适定性，在2D中采用非常弱混合格式并进行形式上的单侧形状线性化以获得状态与伴随系统。

Result: 1D情况下验证了后验良定性和MAP估计的存在性，LMAP近似与MCMC结果高度一致但计算成本显著降低（约O(10^1)次正演模拟）；2D中方法能生成与大集合EnKI相当的重构结果和不确定性估计，且无需针对不同几何或实验配置重新设计反演流程。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了在移动边界条件下对渗透率场的高效、稳定反演，通过代表元方法将更新和协方差计算降维至观测空间，具有良好的可扩展性和通用性，适用于多种几何与实验设置。

Abstract: We develop an infinite-dimensional Bayesian framework for recovering spatially varying log-permeability from pressure observations in single-phase Darcy flow with a moving boundary, motivated by resin transfer moulding. The main contribution is an observation-wise representer formulation linking sensitivities of the parameter-to-observable map to the moving-boundary state and adjoint systems. This yields a reduced Levenberg--Marquardt method for maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimation on the Cameron--Martin space of a Gaussian prior and an explicit finite-rank covariance for the linearised MAP (LMAP) approximation. Once the representers are computed, the LM update and covariance assembly reduce to linear algebra in observation space, independently of the discretisation dimension.
  In 1D, the explicit moving-boundary solution is used to establish posterior well-posedness and existence of MAP estimators, and to derive closed-form Fréchet derivatives and representers. In 2D, we introduce a very weak mixed formulation coupling pressure, interface velocity and domain evolution. A formal one-sided directional shape linearisation yields the corresponding linearised state and adjoint systems, with observation-wise representers computed through independent, parallel adjoint problems.
  Numerical experiments show that the 1D LMAP approximation closely matches a reference MCMC posterior while requiring \(\mathcal{O}(10^1)\) rather than \(\mathcal{O}(10^5\text{--}10^6)\) forward simulations. In 2D, the method produces reconstructions and uncertainty estimates comparable to ensemble Kalman inversion with large ensembles, at substantially lower computational cost. The workflow is demonstrated across several geometries and experimental configurations without reformulating the inversion methodology.

</details>


### [105] [Structured Tensor Approximation from Lateral Slice Sampling via Basis and Manifold Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05463)
*Jeongmin Chae,Usama Saleem,Selin Bac,Shaama Mallikarjun Sharada,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为BMTA的结构化张量逼近方法，结合全局和局部结构先验，利用低秩Tucker分解进行高效重建，并提供了非渐近重构误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅有少量侧向切片可观测的情况下，传统张量逼近方法难以同时捕捉数据沿物理轨迹的全局变化与局部切片间的相关性，因此需要一种能融合全局与局部结构先验的新型逼近方法。

Method: BMTA方法结合了两种信号模型：(i) 捕捉物理轨迹上全局平滑变化的拟基模型；(ii) 描述张量切片间局部关系的流形引导插值模型，并嵌入低秩Tucker重建框架以估计基函数系数并优化张量。

Result: 理论分析给出了非渐近重构误差界，刻画了采样复杂度、优化收敛性和模型失配的影响；在合成数据及量子化学、时空感知等真实数据上的实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: BMTA能有效利用全局与局部结构先验，在有限观测下实现高精度张量逼近，具有良好的理论保证和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we consider a structured tensor approximation problem, where only a limited number of lateral slices are observed. The proposed algorithm , called Basis and Manifold prior Tensor Approximation (BMTA), exploits both global and local structures of the evolution of a global tensor. Specifically, BMTA integrates two signal models: (i) a quasi-basis model that captures smooth global variations along a physical trajectory, and (ii) a manifold-guided interpolation model that characterizes local relationships among tensor slices. A low-rank Tucker reconstruction framework is incorporated to efficiently capture the priors, resulting in coefficients for basis function estimation and a tensor optimization. In addition, we provide a theoretical analysis which establishes a non-asymptotic reconstruction error bound that characterizes the effects of sampling complexity, optimization convergence, and model mismatch. Numerical experiments are performed on both synthetic and real-world datasets, including quantum chemistry and spatiotemporal sensing applications.

</details>


### [106] [A Second-Order Monolithic Scheme for the Coupled Stokes--Biot Model Using Total Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05501)
*Talal Alshehri,Mingchao Cai,Xiaoqin Shen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种二阶全隐式单向时间离散方法，用于求解耦合的瞬态Stokes和准静态Biot系统，具有良好的稳定性和收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在近不可压缩条件下提高鲁棒性并抑制体积锁定，改进Biot系统的数值模拟效果。

Method: 采用三场总压力公式（引入总压力作为额外未知数），结合BDF2时间离散格式，并对整个耦合系统进行单向求解。

Result: 建立了离散能量稳定性，证明了关于Lamé参数、Biot-Willis系数和存储系数的鲁棒性，并获得了时间和空间上的最优收敛阶。数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该方法在能量范数下具有二阶时间精度和最优空间收敛性，适用于复杂耦合多孔弹性问题的高效稳定模拟。

Abstract: We develop and analyze a second-order, fully implicit, monolithic time-discretization for the coupled time-dependent Stokes and quasi-static Biot system. The Biot subsystem is formulated in a three-field total-pressure formulation, in which the total pressure is introduced as an additional unknown together with the solid displacement and pore pressure. This reformulation improves robustness in nearly incompressible regimes and suppresses volumetric locking. All variables are discretized in time using the BDF2 scheme, and the coupled problem is solved monolithically without operator splitting. Discrete energy stability is established using the BDF2 $G$-stability identity. The resulting estimates exhibit robustness with respect to the Lamé parameter, Biot--Willis coefficient, and storage coefficient. A consistency--stability argument is then used to derive second-order convergence in time together with optimal-order convergence in space. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical results and demonstrate second-order temporal accuracy in the corresponding energy norms.

</details>


### [107] [Splitting methods for Intermediate Long Wave and perturbed Benjamin--Ono models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05540)
*Yvonne Alama Bronsard,Clémentine Courtès,Benjamin Melinand*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Birkhoff坐标和显式公式的新型分裂方法，用于数值求解包括ILW方程在内的非线性PDE，并实现了更低正则性要求下的收敛性，适用于高效长时间模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进现有分裂方法对正则性要求过高和时间步长受限的问题，实现对非完全可积方程的高效高精度长期数值模拟。

Method: 利用Benjamin-Ono方程的Birkhoff坐标构造显式公式，并将其推广为一种新的分裂格式，应用于Intermediate Long Wave（ILW）等方程，在仅需初始数据多一阶导数的情况下实现H^s范数下的一阶收敛。

Result: 证明了该方法在H^s范数下对ILW方程具有一阶收敛性且仅需u₀ ∈ H^{s+1}；在深水极限下，ILW的数值解收敛到BO方程的解；相比经典方法，无需严格的二次时间步限制即可近似保持能量，显著提升长期模拟效率。

Conclusion: 该方法结合了可积系统结构与数值逼近优势，为非线性色散方程提供了高效、稳定且低正则性依赖的数值求解方案，适用于研究如孤立子分解猜想等长期动力学行为。

Abstract: Recently, novel integrability techniques, and most notably Birkhoff coordinates followed by an explicit formula, have been introduced to solve the Benjamin--Ono (BO) equation, leading to major theoretical and computational advances. We propose to perturb this explicit BO formula via a novel splitting method for numerically solving a class of PDEs, which includes the Intermediate Long Wave (ILW) equation, as well as other quasilinear equations that are not necessarily integrable. Using the Birkhoff coordinates of BO we establish first-order convergence in the $H^s$-norm ($s \ge 0$) while requiring only one additional derivative on the initial data $u_0 \in H^{s+1}_{0}$, significantly improving upon the regularity requirements of typical splitting methods for nonlinear dispersive equations. Furthermore, we prove that in the deep-water limit, the scheme for the ILW equation converges to the BO solution. Computational advantages of these schemes are shown in simulations: unlike classical splitting methods, our approach does not require a restrictive quadratic time step condition to nearly preserve energy over long time scales, thus rendering efficient and accurate long-time simulations feasible. As an application, we numerically explore the soliton resolution conjecture for both the ILW and a KdV--BO equation.

</details>


### [108] [Minimization-based polynomial corrections for high-order curved boundaries on fixed and moving domains: assessment on finite volume and discontinuous Galerkin schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05768)
*Mirco Ciallella,Walter Boscheri*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种无需求解线性系统即可施加高阶边界条件的新策略，适用于固定和移动的曲面域，结合高阶数值方法实现了高精度模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分段线性三角化网格上对曲面域进行高阶精度模拟时，需同时降低PDE离散误差和几何误差。传统ROD方法虽能实现高阶边界条件，但需求解随多项式阶数和网格细化增长的局部线性系统，计算成本高。

Method: 受一维分析启发，将ROD类方法重构为简单的多项式修正，避免了线性系统的求解。该方法在Runge-Kutta间断Galerkin和ADER任意拉格朗日-欧拉有限体积框架中实现，适用于固定和移动域的可压缩流。

Result: 通过二维和三维的Dirichlet和滑移壁面边界条件数值实验，验证了方法的有效性，收敛阶可达五阶。

Conclusion: 所提策略简化了最小化类边界处理的实现，显著降低了计算成本，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: In this work, we present two novel strategies to impose high-order boundary conditions on fixed and moving curved domains, approximated with piecewise affine triangulations. Achieving high-order accuracy on curved domains requires tackling both the PDE discretization error and the geometrical error simultaneously. While the former can be reduced by employing high-order numerical methods such as finite volume and discontinuous Galerkin, the latter demands either a high-order parametrization of the physical domain or a consistent approximation of the boundary conditions. Minimization-based approaches like the Reconstruction for Off-site Data (ROD) method allow one to skip the construction of high-order curvilinear meshes by defining high-order consistent boundary conditions on a computational boundary that does not match the physical one. The ROD approach mitigates the second-order geometrical error by retrieving a modified polynomial in each boundary cell, which enforces the boundary conditions exactly on the physical boundary. However, the standard ROD method requires the inversion of a local linear system, whose cost grows with the polynomial degree and mesh refinement. Inspired by a recent one-dimensional analysis, we show that ROD-type approaches can be recast as simple polynomial corrections, applicable without any linear system inversion. This greatly simplifies the development of minimization-based boundary treatments and reduces the associated computational cost. To prove the wide applicability of our strategy, we develop it within a Runge-Kutta discontinuous Galerkin framework and an ADER arbitrary-Lagrangian-Eulerian finite volume framework for compressible flows on fixed and moving domains. Several numerical experiments with Dirichlet and slip-wall boundary conditions are presented, with convergence analysis up to fifth order in both 2D and 3D.

</details>


### [109] [Divergence-free interpolation of tangential vector fields via matrix-valued kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05547)
*Zhengjie Sun,Lishuo Dong,Biao Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种在单位球面上对切向量场进行无散核插值的新方法，基于欧氏空间中的标量径向核构造切向、无散的矩阵核，避免了重复应用表面微分算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于势的方法需要高阶正则性且难以灵活设计核函数，本文旨在构建更灵活、低正则性要求的无散核插值方法。

Method: 利用标量径向核构造切向无散矩阵核，结合向量球谐函数推导显式核表示并分析傅里叶乘子，提出逆拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米构造以保持标量核的乘子特性，并在离散节点上建立插值误差估计和最小特征值下界。

Result: 建立了插值矩阵最小特征值的下界，得到了点态和Sobolev空间下的误差估计（对更光滑目标函数具有超收敛性），数值实验验证了理论收敛性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种几何约束与核选择解耦的灵活框架，适用于球面及非球面曲面的无散向量场插值，具有良好的理论保证和数值性能。

Abstract: We develop and analyze a family of divergence-free kernel interpolation methods for tangential vector fields on the unit sphere. Starting from scalar radial kernels in Euclidean space, we construct tangent-valued, surface divergence-free matrix kernels without repeatedly applying surface differential operators. The construction separates the geometric enforcement of the divergence-free constraint from the choice of scalar generator and admits lower-order variants with reduced regularity requirements compared with classical potential-based methods. Using vector spherical harmonics, we derive explicit kernel representations and characterize their Fourier multipliers. We also introduce an inverse Laplace-Beltrami construction that preserves the multipliers of the underlying scalar zonal kernel. For interpolation at scattered nodes, we establish a lower bound for the smallest eigenvalue of the interpolation matrix and derive pointwise and Sobolev error estimates, including superconvergence for targets smoother than the native space. Numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical convergence and stability results, while additional examples on nonspherical surfaces illustrate the applicability of the kernel formula beyond the sphere.

</details>


### [110] [Iterative Decoupling Methods for a Total-Pressure Formulation of Quasi-Static Electroporoelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05566)
*Huipeng Gu,Mingchao Cai,Jingzhi Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种五场耦合的准静态电渗弹性模型，通过引入总压力避免了几乎不可压缩情况下的锁定现象，并设计了具有几何收敛性的迭代解耦方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决传统电渗弹性模型在近不可压缩条件下出现的poroelastic锁定问题，并提高数值计算的稳定性与效率。

Method: 将Maxwell方程与Biot poroelasticity通过电动力学耦合联立，引入总压力重构为五场系统；采用BDF2时间离散和混合有限元空间离散，构建全离散单体格式，并发展一种交替求解电磁与poroelastic子问题的迭代解耦方法。

Result: 提出了一个稳定的五场弱形式及全离散格式，证明了解耦方法在物理耦合条件下具有网格无关的几何收敛性，并提出了代数等价的简化形式以提升计算效率；数值实验验证了理论结果并展示了无锁定性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的五场公式和迭代解耦方法有效解决了电渗弹性问题中的锁定现象，具备良好的稳定性和收敛性，适用于高精度模拟近不可压缩介质中的多物理场耦合行为。

Abstract: Quasi-static electroporoelasticity couples Maxwell's equations with Biot's poroelasticity through electrokinetic coupling between the electric field and the pressure gradient. By introducing the total pressure, the electroporoelasticity equations are reformulated as a five-field system to address poroelastic locking in the nearly incompressible regime. For the resulting five-field system, a monolithic weak formulation is derived, together with a continuous stability estimate. A second-order backward differentiation formula (BDF2) time discretization and a mixed finite- element spatial discretization are then introduced, yieling to a fully discrete monolithic scheme. Building on this scheme, we develop an iterative decoupling method that alternates between an electromagnetic subproblem and a poroelastic subproblem, and prove its geometric convergence to the monolithic solution with an explicit mesh-independent contraction factor under the physical coupling condition. An algebraically equivalent reduced form is also presented, in which the electromagnetic block is solved only once per time step, while the poroelastic block is solved iteratively with electric-field correction updates obtained from the pressure-gradient feedback. Numerical experiments verify the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the locking-free performance.

</details>


### [111] [Efficient higher-order multi-scale method and its convergence estimate for dynamic nonlinear hygro-thermo-mechanical coupling problems of heterogeneous structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05580)
*Yifei Ding,Hao Dong,Jiale Linghu,Yangshuai Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于非线性湿热力耦合问题的高效、高精度、低成本的高阶多尺度（HOMS）计算框架，适用于具有高度空间非均匀性的异质结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于材料属性随温度或湿度变化以及化学反应引起的内部热源和湿气汇项，传统方法难以高效准确模拟非线性湿热力耦合行为，因此需要发展一种新的多尺度方法。

Method: 采用多尺度渐近法结合泰勒级数展开，构建包含高阶修正项的高精度多尺度渐近模型，并设计基于有限差分和有限元的两阶段数值算法（离线与在线阶段），同时严格推导了解的误差估计。

Result: 数值实验表明所提HOMS方法在二维和三维问题中均表现出优异的精度和鲁棒性，且计算开销低；理论误差分析证明了高阶修正项的必要性和方法优越性。

Conclusion: HOMS框架为复杂异质结构中的非线性湿热力耦合问题提供了高效可靠的解决方案，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel higher-order multi-scale (HOMS) computational framework for efficient, high-accuracy, and low-cost simulation of nonlinear hygro-thermo-mechanical (H-T-M) coupling problems in heterogeneous structures. The inherent nonlinearity in the investigated model stems primarily from temperature- or moisture-dependent material properties, and this model also accounts for temperature-dependent internal heat source and moisture sink terms induced by exothermic, moisture-consuming chemical reactions (e.g., hydration). The main contributions of this work are as follows. First, a high-accuracy multi-scale asymptotic model incorporating higher-order correction terms is constructed for nonlinear H-T-M coupling problems in heterogeneous structures with highly spatial inhomogeneity, using the multi-scale asymptotic approach together with Taylor series expansions. Second, rigorous error estimates in both point-wise and integral senses are derived for the multi-scale asymptotic solutions, which theoretically demonstrate the necessity and superiority of the proposed HOMS method. Third, an efficient two-stage numerical algorithm with off-line and on-line stages is developed, based on finite difference and finite element methods, and its convergence is also proved rigorously. Finally, two- and three-dimensional numerical experiments are performed to assess the computational performance of the proposed HOMS approach, showing excellent numerical accuracy and robustness with low computational overhead.

</details>


### [112] [A priori error estimator for reduced-order models based on the higher-order Craig-Bampton method in dynamic substructuring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05625)
*Jaemin Kim,Seung-Hwan Boo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次化的误差估计框架，用于在不求解全阶问题的情况下预测Craig-Bampton（CB）及其高阶形式（HCB）模型降阶方法的特征值误差，利用HCB方法中嵌套的Ritz子空间结构实现先验误差估计，并通过数值算例验证了所提广义CB误差估计器和新型HCB-1误差估计器的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏无需求解全阶模型即可评估降阶模型精度的先验误差估计方法，尤其是在高阶Craig-Bampton（HCB）方法中，尽管其精度更高，但无法预先判断误差，限制了其实用性。因此，亟需一种可在不依赖全阶解的前提下准确估计特征值误差的方法。

Method: 提出一个基于HCB方法嵌套Ritz子空间结构的层次化误差估计框架：（i）通过Rayleigh商扰动分析推导出广义CB误差估计器；（ii）利用HCB-2的特征解作为参考，构建新型HCB-1误差估计器。该方法利用高一阶的HCB解作为低一阶解的参考，实现对前一阶误差的估计。

Result: 在多个几何复杂度不同的数值算例中验证了所提出的两个误差估计器的有效性，结果表明该框架能够准确预测CB和HCB-1方法的特征值误差，且无需求解全阶特征值问题，显著提升了模型降阶方法的实用性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 本文成功填补了HCB方法缺乏先验误差估计器的空白，提出的层次化估计框架不仅适用于标准CB方法，还可扩展至高阶HCB方法，为基于组件模态综合的动态子结构技术提供了可靠的误差评估手段。

Abstract: The Craig-Bampton (CB) method is a widely used dynamic substructuring technique based on component mode synthesis (CMS). The higher-order Craig-Bampton (HCB) method augments the CB basis with residual modes from a Neumann series expansion of the residual flexibility matrix, where HCB-n retains terms up to the n-th order and is reduced back to the CB size via the System Equivalent Reduction Expansion Process (SEREP), achieving improved accuracy at the same model dimension. However, assessing the accuracy of a reduced model without solving the full-order problem remains a fundamental challenge: if the full-order solution is required to evaluate the error, the purpose of model reduction is defeated. Despite the demonstrated superiority of the HCB method, no a priori error estimator (one that predicts eigenvalue errors without solving the full-order eigenvalue problem) has been proposed for it. The present work addresses this gap with a hierarchical estimation framework that exploits the nested Ritz subspace structure of the HCB method, where each higher-order solution serves as a reference for estimating the error of the preceding order. The framework provides (i) a generalized CB error estimator derived from a Rayleigh quotient perturbation analysis, and (ii) a novel HCB-1 error estimator using the HCB-2 eigensolution as a reference. Numerical examples across models of varying geometric complexity validate both estimators.

</details>


### [113] [A Kernel Formula for Kinetic Fokker-Planck Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05770)
*Fuqun Han,Wuchen Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种显式单步核公式来近似求解动力学Fokker-Planck方程，基于参考动力学和加权相空间中的扰动方法，并展示了其在数值实验中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更有效地逼近动力学Fokker-Planck方程的密度演化，需要一种具有明确结构且便于数值实现的核方法。

Method: 从具有显式转移核的参考动力学出发，引入精心选择的辅助权重以编码漂移扰动，构建显式的单步核公式，并在加权相空间中分析其局部弱一致性和收敛性。

Result: 得到了一个显式的核公式，证明了其局部弱一致性与一阶有限时间弱收敛性，并给出了正则化Wasserstein型近端算子的变分解释，同时推广到更广泛的抛物型方程。

Conclusion: 该核公式为动力学Fokker-Planck方程提供了一个有效且具理论保证的显式逼近方法，适用于确定性粒子算法等数值应用。

Abstract: We derive an explicit one-step kernel formula for the kinetic Fokker-Planck equation. The construction begins with a reference dynamics admitting an explicit transition kernel and uses a carefully chosen auxiliary weight to encode the drift perturbation. The resulting kernel formula provides an explicit approximation of the density evolution. In a weighted phase-space setting, we establish local weak consistency and first-order finite-time weak convergence under suitable regularity and moment assumptions. We also give a formal variational interpretation of the formula in terms of a regularized Wasserstein-type proximal operator and present analogous constructions for a broader class of parabolic equations. Numerical experiments illustrate the accuracy of the kernel approximation and its application to deterministic particle schemes for kinetic sampling.

</details>


### [114] [A neural operator view on U-Nets for inverse imaging problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05839)
*Alexander Auras,Martin Burger,Samira Kabri,Michael Moeller,Michael Schopf-Kuester*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了U形神经算子架构在解决离散化分辨率变化时的逆成像问题中的表现，发现尽管设计上具有分辨率不变性，传统U-Net架构在分辨率变化下比预期更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨深度神经网络在离散化分辨率趋于无限时对不适定逆问题的求解能力，特别是神经算子在不同分辨率下的泛化性能。

Method: 回顾了类似U-Net的神经算子学习方法，使用1D玩具模型增强可解释性，并在有限角度CT重建任务中进行大量数值实验，评估不同神经算子U-Net在不同分辨率下的泛化能力。

Result: 实验表明，虽然U形神经算子架构在设计上具备分辨率不变性，但传统的U-Net架构在面对分辨率变化时表现出意料之外的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 传统U-Net在分辨率泛化方面表现优于预期，提示其结构可能隐含某种适应性机制，值得进一步研究以提升神经算子在真实不适定问题中的应用。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have shown great empirical success in the solution of a wide variety of ill-posed inverse problems in imaging. Yet, very few works have studied their behavior in the limit that turns the discretized ill-conditioned problems into truly ill-posed ones, i.e., for an increasing resolution of the discretization. In this work, we review common approaches to neural operator learning in architectures that resemble a U-Net, one of the most common classical architectures for inverse imaging problems. We discuss advantages and drawbacks of the respective approaches, consider a 1D toy example for improved interpretability, and present extensive numerical experiments on how different types of neural operator U-Nets can improve a first (crude) limited angle CT-reconstruction. In particular, we study how well networks trained for a certain resolution of the discretization generalize to other resolutions. Our finding is that while U-shaped neural operator architectures are by design resolution-invariant, the classical U-Net architecture seems to be more robust with respect to resolution changes than expected.

</details>


### [115] [Rothe time discretization and weak solutions for a cutoff Westervelt system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05865)
*Marvin Fritz*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Westervelt方程的一种截断一阶形式的完全隐式Rothe时间离散化方法，通过引入焓变量和原始迁移率变量，将每个非线性时间步转化为一致单调椭圆问题，并证明了格式的良好适定性和弱解的存在性，同时提供了数值实验验证其稳定性和时间收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服高阶能量估计和逆不等式带来的困难，研究一种更稳健的时间离散化方法来求解Westervelt方程的截断一阶形式。

Method: 采用完全隐式的Rothe方法进行时间离散化，引入焓变量和原始迁移率变量使每一步的非线性问题转化为均匀单调椭圆问题，利用Browder-Minty定理证明格式的适定性，并通过Alt-Luckhaus型论证建立紧性以取极限得到弱解存在性。

Result: 证明了Rothe格式的适定性、离散能量不等式成立、变换变量的紧性以及弱解的存在性；提出了弱强唯一性原理和截断项不活跃的后验条件判据；给出了强制截断问题在正则解下的相容性估计，并通过数值实验验证了格式的稳定性及近似一阶时间精度。

Conclusion: 该方法有效避免了传统方法中的高阶估计难题，为Westervelt方程提供了一种稳定且具理论基础的数值求解方案，在适当条件下截断操作不影响解的准确性。

Abstract: We study a fully implicit Rothe time discretization for a cutoff first-order formulation of the Westervelt equation. The key ingredients are the enthalpy variable and the primitive mobility variable, which turn each nonlinear time step into a uniformly monotone elliptic problem and avoid higher-order energy estimates and inverse inequalities. For every time step, the discrete problem reduces to a monotone elliptic equation, which yields well-posedness of the Rothe scheme by the Browder-Minty theorem. We derive a discrete energy inequality, establish compactness for the transformed variable by an Alt-Luckhaus type argument, and pass to the limit to obtain existence of weak solutions for the cutoff first-order system. We prove a weak-strong uniqueness principle for the cutoff problem and formulate a conditional a posteriori criterion under which the cutoff is inactive. For sufficiently regular solutions of the forced cutoff problem, we establish consistency estimates which identify the temporal residuals produced by the Rothe approximation in the natural discrete-in-time weak norms. These estimates provide a rigorous consistency basis for the observed temporal behavior. Finally, numerical experiments illustrate the stability of the scheme, its observed near first-order temporal behavior, and inactivity of the cutoff along the computed discrete trajectories in the tested regimes.

</details>


### [116] [Reduced Basis Method for Simulating Thermal Transients in Electric Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05904)
*Herbert Egger,Eva-Maria Haslhofer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文讨论了使用降阶基（RB）方法对感应电机瞬态热仿真进行建模，其精度可与有限元（FE）模型媲美，且具有类似集总参数热网络（LPTN）的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在保证高精度的同时提高计算效率，需要一种能够快速、自动构建的降阶模型来替代复杂的有限元模型用于感应电机的热仿真。

Method: 采用降阶基（RB）方法构建瞬态热仿真模型，并将其与经过实测校准的有限元模型和集总参数热网络（LPTN）进行性能对比。

Result: 数值结果表明，RB模型可以达到与有限元模型相当的精度，支持完全自动化的构建过程，并且计算效率与传统的LPTN相当。

Conclusion: RB方法在保持高精度的同时实现了高效计算和自动化建模，是感应电机热仿真中一种优于传统方法的可行方案。

Abstract: Reduced Basis (RB) methods provide low-dimensional approximations of parametrized partial differential equations with controllable accuracy. We discuss the construction of RB approximations for transient thermal simulation of an induction motor and compare their performance to Finite Element (FE) models and Lumped Parameter Thermal Networks (LPTNs) after calibration to measurements. Numerical results demonstrate that RB models can achieve FE accuracy, allow for a fully automatic construction, and offer computational efficiency comparable to traditional LPTNs.

</details>


### [117] [A Thermodynamically Consistent Cahn-Hilliard-Navier-Stokes Model for Tumor Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06099)
*Chenyang Li,Ping Lin,Hui Yu,Haibiao Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于能量变分框架的热力学一致相场模型，用于模拟肿瘤生长，并设计了高效、稳定且质量守恒的时间离散格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了准确模拟肿瘤生长过程中多物理场耦合的复杂动力学行为，并保持热力学一致性。

Method: 结合Cahn-Hilliard方程与不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，采用基于MSAV方法和压力校正策略的一阶时间离散格式。

Result: 所提格式被严格证明是无条件能量稳定且质量守恒的，并建立了最优一阶时间误差估计；数值实验验证了方法的有效性与收敛性。

Conclusion: 该模型和数值方法能有效模拟肿瘤生长，具备良好的稳定性、精度和理论支撑，适用于复杂生物系统的建模与仿真。

Abstract: This work develops a thermodynamically consistent phase-field model for tumor growth based on the energetic variational framework. The model couples the Cahn-Hilliard equations for tumor evolution and nutrient transport with the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations. A first-order time discretization scheme based on the Multiple Scalar Auxiliary Variables (MSAV) approach together with a pressure-correction strategy is proposed to efficiently handle the nonlinear and coupled structure of the system. The proposed scheme is rigorously proved to be unconditionally energy stable and mass conservative. Furthermore, optimal first-order temporal error estimates are established for the tumor phase-field variable, the nutrient concentration, and the fluid velocity. Finally, numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method and verify the theoretical convergence rates.

</details>


### [118] [ChromOps.jl: High-order simulation and discrete forward sensitivity analysis for chromatography models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06131)
*Kristian Meyer,Maksym Ratajczyk,Christopher Rackauckas*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于Julia语言的可微分色谱求解器ChromOps.jl，结合高阶空间离散化与离散前向敏感性分析（DFSA），有效提升了色谱过程建模中参数估计与优化的效率和易用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统色谱模型在梯度优化中受限于计算成本和目标函数梯度推导的复杂性，因此需要一种高效且易于实现的可微分求解方法。

Method: 开发了ChromOps.jl求解器，采用有限差分求和分部（FD-SBP）和间断伽辽金谱元法（DG-SEM）两种高阶空间离散化方法，并结合ForwardDiff.jl实现离散前向敏感性分析（DFSA），统一处理原问题与对偶问题的计算路径。

Result: 在6组分离子交换色谱问题上，两种方法均达到理论收敛阶数，FD-SBP在正向和对偶模拟中表现出更优的功耗精度性能且更易实现；DFSA的计算开销约为每增加一个参数需1.4倍正向求解时间，且无需手动推导梯度代码即可提供精确梯度。

Conclusion: ChromOps.jl通过高阶离散化与自动微分技术，实现了高效、准确且易于使用的梯度计算，显著促进了机理性色谱模型的参数估计与优化。

Abstract: Mechanistic chromatography models are valuable for process development, but gradient-based parameter estimation and optimization can be hindered by computational cost and the effort of deriving objective-function gradients. To address this concern, a fully differentiable Julia chromatography solver, ChromOps.jl, is presented that combines high-order spatial discretization with discrete forward sensitivity analysis (DFSA). Two high-order spatial discretizations, finite difference summation-by-parts (FD-SBP) and the discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method (DG-SEM), are compared on a 6-component ion-exchange chromatography problem with steric mass action kinetics. Both attain their theoretical convergence rates on manufactured problems, while FD-SBP shows favourable work-precision performance for both forward (primal) and dual-valued simulations and is conceptually simpler to implement. DFSA scales as tDFSA approx 1.4(1+Np)tfwd for up to Np = 24 parameters propagated in a single ForwardDiff.jl chunk, i.e. about 1.4 forward solves per additional parameter. Because the primal and dual-valued solves share the same code path, DFSA provides accurate gradients of user-defined objectives without any hand-derived chain-rule code, enabling accessible gradient-based parameter estimation and optimization.

</details>


### [119] [Gradient Descent on Point Clouds and Applications in Learned Operator Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06267)
*Andreas Hauptmann,Yury Korolev,Matthew Thorpe*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种在由点云隐式给出的未知流形上最小化能量的梯度下降方法，在适当假设下可收敛到局部极小值附近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已知流形上已有类似欧氏空间的梯度下降方法，但当流形未知时需同时估计流形并优化能量函数。

Method: 定义了一种保持在流形邻域内的梯度下降方案，并结合稳定性与采样假设进行分析。

Result: 该方法在时间步长和采样误差趋于零时，收敛到局部极小值的一个邻域，且该邻域大小也趋于零。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效处理基于点云数据的流形上的优化问题，并可用于逆问题中的算子修正学习等应用。

Abstract: We consider the problem of minimising an energy over an unknown manifold that is given implicitly by a point cloud. For a known manifold one can define a gradient descent scheme analogously to the classical construction in Euclidean spaces. However, when the manifold is not known one has to simultaneously estimate the manifold whilst minimising the energy. We define a gradient descent scheme which remains in a neighbourhood of the manifold and, under suitable stability and sampling assumptions, converges to a neighbourhood of a local minimiser whose size vanishes as the time step and sampling errors vanish. As an example we show the application of the methodology to learning operator corrections in inverse problems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Nucleation beyond Equilibrium: Fronts Control Invasion in Bistable Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05251)
*Victor Lequin,Giulio Biroli,Camille Scalliet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于非平衡态反应-扩散系统中具有非守恒向量序参量的成核理论，推广了经典成核理论，并在生态学Lotka-Volterra模型中验证了强种间竞争会显著增加入侵难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于生态动力学通常不满足细致平衡条件，且涉及多个耦合的非守恒变量，缺乏普适的成核理论，因此需要发展新的理论框架来描述此类系统的双稳态转变过程。

Method: 推导了适用于非守恒向量序参量的反应-扩散系统的成核理论，结合前沿动力学（如前端速度和扩散系数）替代传统能量项，并通过大量数值计算拟势来验证理论在近双结线和自旋odal区域的有效性。

Result: 发现经典成核理论的数学结构在非平衡系统中依然成立，只需将体自由能差和表面张力分别替换为前端速度和扩散系数；在Lotka-Volterra模型中揭示了强种间竞争导致前缘出现丰度显著下降的耗尽区，使入侵变得指数级困难。

Conclusion: 该理论成功将成核过程的理解扩展到非平衡生态系统，揭示了向量序参量的重要性，并预测可通过微生物实验检验的竞争强度效应。

Abstract: Bistability, the existence of two alternative stable states with distinct basins of attraction, is common across ecology and many other biological, chemical, and physical systems. In spatially extended systems, the invasion of one state by the other proceeds through nucleation, the fluctuation-driven growth of a droplet beyond a critical size. Classical Nucleation Theory (CNT) quantifies this process using the energy landscape, but this description relies on detailed balance, the condition of microscopic reversibility that holds at equilibrium. Ecological dynamics generally violate detailed balance and are described not by a single scalar field but by several coupled, non-conserved abundances--a vector order parameter--for which no general nucleation theory exists. Here we derive such a theory for reaction-diffusion systems with a non-conserved vector order parameter, valid both close to the binodal, where invasion proceeds through propagating fronts, and close to the spinodal, where the metastable state loses stability. Extensive numerical computations of the quasipotential confirm the theory in both regimes. We show that the mathematical structure of CNT survives out of equilibrium, once energetic quantities are replaced by dynamical properties of fronts: the front speed plays the role of the bulk free-energy difference between phases, and the diffusivity that of the surface tension. Applied to the two-species Lotka-Volterra model, an archetypal system of bistable ecological antagonism, our theory shows that strong interspecific competition generates a pronounced depletion region within fronts, where the total abundance falls well below carrying capacity. This vectorial structure, invisible to a scalar description based on species frequency alone, makes invasion exponentially harder as competition strengthens at fixed competitive advantage--a prediction testable in microbial systems.

</details>


### [121] [Theory of Measurement-Altered Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05289)
*Kabir Khanna,Sara Murciano,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监测Tomomaga-Luttinger液体的理论，研究了局部测量如何改变无能隙量子物质中的长程关联。通过复制瞬子计算和矩阵乘积态模拟，揭示了测量诱导随机性导致关联函数出现普适幂律衰减及对数修正，并展示了临界量子态在物理测量下的新标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索局部测量对一维量子临界态中长程关联的影响，特别是在弱监测条件下如何改变传统的临界标度行为。

Method: 发展了基于复制瞬子的解析方法来处理Born平均下的可观测量，并结合矩阵乘积态（MPS）数值计算进行验证。

Result: 发现当测量具有相关性时，密度和相位涨落的平均关联函数在长距离下表现为带有对数修正的普适幂律衰减；揭示了关联函数各阶矩的多重分形谱，表现出强烈非高斯的涨落特征。

Conclusion: 物理测量会以超越强制测量和传统临界标度的方式改变量子临界态，建立了测量诱导随机性下新的临界现象理论框架。

Abstract: Local measurements can alter long-range correlations in gapless quantum matter. We propose a theory of weakly-monitored Tomonaga-Luttinger liquids, a broad class of quantum critical states in one dimension. In order to address the intrinsic randomness of the measurement record, we develop a replica instanton calculation to study Born-averaged observables. We find that when measurements are relevant, average correlators of density and phase fluctuations decay at long distances as universal power laws with logarithmic corrections, a feature we argue is peculiar to measurement-induced randomness. We characterize the full multifractal spectrum of moments of correlations functions, revealing broad, strongly non-gaussian fluctuations across the ensemble of post-measurement states. We support these analytic results with matrix-product-state calculations, and provide a general picture of measurement-altered criticality for ground states described by 1+1d conformal field theories. Our results establish that physical measurements alter critical quantum states in a manner that lies beyond both forced measurements and conventional critical scaling.

</details>


### [122] [Thermodynamic statistics of given names in USA and France](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06048)
*Klaus M. Frahm,Dima L. Shepelyansky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过分析美国和法国100多年的姓名分布数据，发现其不平等程度（以基尼系数0.85-0.95为特征）与财富分配相似，并可用物理中的瑞利-金斯热化和凝聚现象来描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究长期社会文化现象中姓名流行度分布的稳定性及其与财富不平等的类比关系。

Method: 利用政府官方数据集，采用洛伦兹曲线、帕累托曲线及基尼系数分析姓名频率分布，并类比物理系统中的瑞利-金斯热化模型进行解释。

Result: 姓名分布的洛伦兹曲线稳定，基尼系数维持在0.85–0.95之间；分布规律符合瑞利-金斯热化和凝聚现象；时间相关性显示20世纪中期前后出现显著变化。

Conclusion: 姓名流行度的长期分布表现出高度不平等且相对稳定，可用物理系统的热化机制建模，揭示了社会命名行为中潜在的普适性规律。

Abstract: Using official government data sets of USA and France we analyze the occurrence/frequency/popularity distributions of given names on a time scale of more than 100 years. These distributions are characterized through the Lorenz and Pareto curves broadly used in the analysis of wealth inequality in the world. These curves remain stable during the considered time period with the Gini coefficient remaining in the narrow range 0.85-0.95. As for the case of wealth inequality, we show that the distributions of names are well described by the Rayleigh-Jeans (RJ) thermalization and condensation phenomenon well studied in various physical systems. The RJ thermalization results from two integrals of motion being analogous to energy and probability norm conservation in physical systems with energy states corresponding to popularity levels of names. Time correlations between names are also determined showing their stability until the middle of the twentieth century and a significant change after that.

</details>


### [123] [Superfluid helium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05354)
*Charles Radin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一个数学上可解的玩具模型，用于描述氦-4从正常液态到超流态的相变，并展示如何获得超流态的序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解超流氦-4的相变机制并确定其序参量。

Method: 构建了一个数学上可解的玩具模型来模拟正常液态与超流态之间的相变过程。

Result: 模型成功展示了如何在理论上获得超流态的序参量。

Conclusion: 该玩具模型为研究超流氦-4的相变提供了理论框架，并有助于揭示其序参量的本质。

Abstract: We are interested in modelling superfluid helium-4, the common isotope of helium, of atomic number 4. We present a mathematically solvable toy model of the phase transition between the normal liquid and superfluid phases and use it to show how an order parameter might be obtained for the superfluid.

</details>


### [124] [Morphology of frozen labyrinths from irreversible threshold dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05496)
*Daniel Richard Levy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究不可逆多数阈值动力学在加权邻域中如何导致非共识迷宫态的冻结，揭示其与可逆动态的本质差异及界面受挫机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当多数阈值动力学变为不可逆时，系统如何从随机初始条件演化并形成稳定非共识结构，理解其与可逆情况的根本区别。

Method: 采用高斯核在晶格上进行不可逆单次翻转规则模拟，分析不同更新顺序对模式形成的影响，并通过干预实验确定决定性粗粒化结构和界面动态机制。

Result: 发现不可逆规则下系统会冻结成平衡但非共识的迷宫态，形态表现为双连续结构，特征宽度亚线性增长且小于相互作用范围；界面受挫现象集中于边界，几乎不携带大尺度形状信息。

Conclusion: 不可逆性改变了多数阈值动力学的结果，导致 arrested coarsening 和确定性主导的图案形成机制，界面受挫是该类系统独有的标志性特征。

Abstract: Majority threshold dynamics, in which each agent adopts the dominant state in a weighted neighborhood, relaxes a binary field toward consensus or stripes. We study what happens when this rule is made irreversible: each agent, interacting through a Gaussian kernel on a lattice, may flip out of its local weighted minority at most once. The reversible form is threshold dynamics of Merriman-Bence-Osher type, an exactly solvable calibration in which interfaces move by mean curvature with closed-form lattice pinning and mobility. Irreversibility changes the outcome. From random initial conditions, the one-flip rule freezes balanced non-consensus labyrinths that reversible relaxation drives away. The patterned regime is a window of initial spin compositions around equal balance, narrowing as the interaction range grows, controlled by a standardized bias whose onset is independent of scale over a fourfold range. The frozen morphology is arrested coarsening: bicontinuous at balance, with a feature width that grows sublinearly and falls below the interaction range at large scales. We determine the mechanism by intervention. Fixing the initial condition while varying the update order shows that the coarse domain layout is deterministic, while the update order enters only in a secondary first-passage race that fine-tunes the wall positions the deterministic dynamics has already set. Those walls are marked by frustration: agents frozen against their own local field. This signature is identically absent from any reversible relaxation, is overwhelmingly interfacial, and carries almost none of the pattern's large-scale shape.

</details>


### [125] [Entanglement Scaling and Full Counting Statistics in Excited States of Two-Dimensional Rotating Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05722)
*Priyangshu Goswami,Abhishek Dhar,Satya N. Majumdar,Anupam Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了二维旋转谐波势阱中一类N粒子费米子激发态的纠缠熵，发现即使在激发态下面积定律仍然成立，并推导了环形区域中Rényi熵和粒子数涨落的可加性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非基态下费米子系统的纠缠熵行为，特别是激发态是否仍满足面积定律，并推广到更复杂几何区域（如环形）中的熵性质。

Method: 通过填充特定单粒子能级构建N粒子激发态，解析计算圆盘区域内阶数为q的Rényi熵及粒子数涨落的累积量，并推导中心化累积量生成函数；进一步将结果推广至环形区域。

Result: 证明了纠缠熵的面积定律在该类激发态中依然成立；验证了非相互作用费米子系统纠缠熵可用粒子数累积量展开的关系；得到了与基态具有相同标度性质的概率分布函数（仅存在变量平移）；在足够宽的环形区域中，Rényi熵和粒子数累积量可分解为两个边界圆盘对应量的和。

Conclusion: 激发态费米子系统在二维旋转谐波势中仍遵循纠缠熵的面积定律，且其统计特性与基态具有相似的标度行为；环形区域中的熵和涨落表现出可加性，表明几何分解方法在此类系统中有效。

Abstract: We investigate the entanglement entropy of a class of $N$-particle excited state of fermions confined in a two-dimensional harmonic trap rotating at an angular frequency $Ω$. The excited state is constructed by filling a particular set of $N$ single-particle energy levels. We analytically compute the Rényi entropies of order $q$ in a disc of radius $r$ around the centre of the trap, and the cumulants corresponding to number fluctuations of fermions within the disc. We found that the area law scaling of entanglement entropy holds even for a class of excited states. We also verified the well-known series expansion of entanglement entropy in terms of the particle number cumulants for non-interacting fermions. We further derive the centered cumulant generating function, demonstrating that the associated probability distribution function in the disc has identical scaling properties, up to a variable shift, to the known ground-state result. Finally, we extend our result to an annular region, showing that both the Rényi entropy and particle number cumulants of the annulus decompose into sums of the corresponding quantities of the two bounding discs. These additive relations hold as long as the width of the annulus is sufficiently large.

</details>


### [126] [Computing Shear Viscosities from Molecular Dynamics Simulation: Comparing the OrthoBoXY Approach with the Green-Kubo Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05845)
*Marcel Brandt,Ralf Ludwig,Dietmar Paschek*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究通过分子动力学模拟使用OrthoBoXY方法计算了15种纯净分子液体的剪切粘度，并与Green-Kubo方法结果进行对比，发现两者高度一致。研究表明，分子液体的粘度在低至250个分子的小系统中不受有限尺寸效应影响，且标准误差几乎与系统大小无关。因此，建议采用更小系统和更长模拟时间以提高效率。此外，研究优化了OrthoBoXY模拟的分块长度τ_block，对高粘性、中等粘性和高流动性系统分别提出1/8、1/4和不缩放的建议，结合小系统可实现最高达24倍的计算成本降低而不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确高效地计算分子液体的剪切粘度是理解其输运性质的关键。传统方法如Green-Kubo需要长时间模拟，计算成本高。OrthoBoXY是一种新兴方法，但其在不同系统大小下的表现以及参数设置（如分块长度）尚需系统研究以避免计算陷阱并提升效率。

Method: 采用平衡态分子动力学（MD）模拟，利用OrthoBoXY方法计算15种纯净分子液体的剪切粘度，并与Green-Kubo方法结果对比。通过多个不同系统大小（最小至250分子）的模拟，分析有限尺寸效应对粘度及其误差的影响机制。基于不同运行长度的数据，优化OrthoBoXY方法中分块长度τ_block的选取规则。

Result: 1. OrthoBoXY与Green-Kubo方法计算的粘度结果高度一致；2. 分子液体的粘度在系统小至250分子时仍无显著有限尺寸效应；3. 粘度的标准误差几乎不随系统大小变化，归因于自扩散系数精度提升与OrthoBoXY加权方案的补偿效应；4. 提出改进的τ_block选取策略：高粘性系统可缩小至1/8，中等粘性至1/4，高流动性系统不应缩小；5. 结合小系统（250分子）与优化参数，计算成本最高可降低24倍而不牺牲精度。

Conclusion: OrthoBoXY方法在小系统中即可准确计算分子液体剪切粘度，且通过优化分块长度τ_block可大幅降低计算成本。推荐采用较小系统配合更长模拟时间，并根据体系粘性程度合理调整τ_block，以实现高效可靠的粘度预测。

Abstract: We calculated shear viscosities of 15 neat molecular liquids from equilibrium molecular dynamics (MD) simulations using the OrthoBoXY approach and compare them to viscosities calculated via the Green-Kubo method. Data from both methods agree very well. Here, we show how to avoid pitfalls while computing the OrthoBoXY-data to obtain optimal results. From simulations of multiple system sizes, we could verify that the viscosity of molecular liquids is not influenced by finite size effects down to systems as small as 250 molecules. Moreover, we demonstrate that also the standard error of the viscosity is nearly independent of the system size. This is shown to be a consequence of a compensation effect of an increasing accuracy of the self-diffusion coefficients with increasing systems-size and the system-size dependent weighting according to the OrthoBoXY-equation. As a consequence, we suggest that it is preferable to run simulations of smaller systems with longer simulation times rather than larger systems with shorter simulation runs. In addition, we discuss a refinement of the recently introduced "recipe" for OrthoBoXY simulations block-lengths $τ_\mathrm{block}$. Based on data from simulations with varying run-lengths, we suggest the following modification: for highly viscous systems, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ might safely be scaled by a factor of $1/8$, significantly reducing the computational resources needed. For less viscous systems, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ might safely be scaled by a factor of $1/4$. For systems with high fluidity, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ should not be scaled down in order to achieve reliable results. When using a smaller system size of 250 molecules, these refinements are leading up to a 24-fold reduction in computational cost compared to the previous recommended set-up without sacrificing numerical accuracy.

</details>


### [127] [Dynamic scaling behavior in the presence of a periodic magnetic driving across Ising continuous transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05936)
*Andrea Pelissetto,Ettore Vicari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究周期性外场驱动下二维伊辛模型在临界温度处的动力学行为，发现磁化强度和键能密度呈现随缩放变量τ和σ振荡的动态标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索连续相变中周期性外场与序参量耦合导致的临界动力学现象。

Method: 基于纯弛豫动力学模拟二维伊辛模型在周期性磁场h(t) = -A cos(2πt/P)作用下的演化，分析磁化与能量的动力学标度律。

Result: 发现系统存在由τ = t/P 和 σ = A P^κ 决定的动态标度行为，其中κ ≈ 0.865；磁化和键能密度在趋于稳态前呈现围绕光滑曲线的振荡行为。

Conclusion: 周期性驱动引发非平庸的动态标度现象，揭示了时间、场幅值与周期之间的非平凡关联，拓展了对驱动-响应系统临界动力学的理解。

Abstract: We study the critical dynamics arising from a time-dependent periodic homogenous source coupled to the order-parameter field, which drives a classical ferromagnetic system across a continuous transition. For this purpose, we consider the paradigmatic two-dimensional (2D) Ising model in the presence of a periodic magnetic field $h(t)=-A\, \cos (2πt/P)$, evolving under a purely relaxational dynamics at the critical temperature. We show that the periodic driving gives rise to a peculiar dynamic scaling behavior in the thermodynamic limit, arising from a nontrivial interplay among the time $t$, the amplitude $A$ and period $P$ of $h(t)$. The relevant scaling variables are $τ=t/P$ and $σ=A P^κ$, with $κ= y_h/z$, where $y_h=(d+2-η)/2$ is the critical dimension of the magnetic field, and $z$ is dynamic exponent for the critical relaxational dynamics ($κ\approx 0.865$ for the 2D Ising model). The dynamic scaling behaviors of the magnetization and bond-energy density show an oscillatory behavior around a smooth curve which approaches a large-$τ$ stationary behavior. We also briefly discuss the dynamic behavior of an Ising system driven across the critical point by a periodic time-varying temperature at zero magnetic field.

</details>


### [128] [Measurement-induced entanglement Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06006)
*Viktor Eisler,Erik Tonni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了无限跃迁链在占据基部分投影测量后其基态的纠缠哈密顿量，发现约化密度矩阵可通过共形映射和规范变换与巨正则系相关联，纠缠哈密顿量具有局域的逆温度和与诱导电荷密度相关的化学势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在部分投影测量后量子系统纠缠结构的变化，特别是纠缠哈密顿量如何反映测量结果的信息。

Method: 利用共形映射和规范变换结合场论描述，分析测量后的约化密度矩阵及其对应的纠缠哈密顿量。

Result: 纠缠哈密顿量的局域逆温度在端点处呈平方根消失且与测量结果无关，而局域化学势与段内的诱导电荷密度相关。

Conclusion: 后选择态的纠缠哈密顿量比纠缠熵包含更多关于测量结果的信息。

Abstract: We study the entanglement Hamiltonian of an infinite hopping chain in its ground state, after partial projective measurements in the occupation basis. For a segment separated by two measurement regions from the rest of the chain, we show that the reduced density matrix can be related to a grand-canonical state via a conformal mapping and a gauge transformation in the underlying field-theory description. The entanglement Hamiltonian is then described by a local inverse temperature that vanishes as a square root around the endpoints and is independent of the particular measurement outcome. In sharp contrast, the local chemical potential is shown to be related to the induced charge density in the segment. Hence the entanglement Hamiltonian of a post-selected state contains much more information on the measurement outcome than the respective entropy.

</details>


### [129] [Collective search-and-capture under competing assignment policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06084)
*Néstor Sepúlveda*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了一种最小格子模型中的主动搜索与捕获过程，发现捕获时间受分配策略影响大于行走者的持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在持久随机游走者定位并不可逆地捕获静止目标的过程中，重定向率和搜索半径对集体完成时间的影响。

Method: 通过测量不同重定向率α和搜索半径R下的集体完成时间Tc，并比较单轮分配、级联重新分配和最大基数匹配三种策略的效果。

Result: Tc(α)呈非单调依赖关系，在中等持久性时达到最小值；最大基数匹配在中等到大搜索半径下显著缩短捕获时间，效果优于两种贪婪策略。

Conclusion: 在此类集体、耗尽耦合的搜索问题中，分配策略对捕获时间的控制作用强于行走者的持久性。

Abstract: We study a minimal lattice model of active search-and-capture in which persistent random walkers locate and irreversibly capture immobile targets through a finite-range, mutually exclusive assignment rule. We measure the collective completion time $T_c$ as a function of the walkers' reorientation rate $α$ and the search radius $R$. The dependence $T_c(α)$ is non-monotonic, with a minimum at intermediate persistence whose depth decreases as $R$ grows. Capture kinetics show that $T_c$ is not a typical capture time but is governed by the extreme, late-time tail of the capture process, while the bulk of targets are captured much earlier; this tail is controlled mainly by the free-exploration phase rather than by the final directed approach. We then compare the baseline single-round assignment rule with cascading reassignment and with maximum-cardinality matching on a candidate graph. The two greedy policies (single-round and cascading) agree at very small $R$, whereas maximum-cardinality matching already produces a strong speedup at moderate $R$: improved matching reduces $T_c$ by factors of several at large $R$, and by more than an order of magnitude at moderate $R$. Thus, in this collective, depletion-coupled search problem, the assignment policy can control the capture time more strongly than the walkers' persistence.

</details>


### [130] [Transport coefficients in hard-sphere fluids: thermodynamic versus kinetic descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06143)
*Miguel Hoyuelos*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于热力学的硬球流体输运系数理论，通过昂萨格矩阵和物态方程预测输运系数与稀薄气体值之比，结果在全流体范围内与模拟数据吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在发展一种仅依赖平衡态热力学性质的输运系数理论，避免复杂的动力学模型。

Method: 从昂萨格矩阵的一般表达式出发，结合物态方程推导出热导率、粘度和自扩散系数的紧凑表达式。

Result: 理论成功预测了σ/σ_id比值，在几乎整个流体范围内与模拟数据定量一致，尤其自扩散系数显著优于恩斯科格动理学理论的预测。

Conclusion: 输运系数可以通过纯热力学框架进行准确描述，表明热力学方法在输运现象中的有效性。

Abstract: A thermodynamic theory for transport coefficients in hard-sphere fluids is developed from a general expression for the Onsager matrix. The theory predicts the ratio $σ/σ_{\rm id}$, where $σ$ is a transport coefficient and $σ_{\rm id}$ denotes its dilute-gas value. This ratio depends exclusively on equilibrium thermodynamic properties and can therefore be computed directly from the equation of state. Compact expressions are obtained for the thermal conductivity, viscosity, and self-diffusion coefficient. These expressions quantitatively reproduce simulation data over almost the entire fluid range and, in the case of self-diffusion, significantly improve upon the predictions of Enskog kinetic theory. The results demonstrate that transport coefficients can be accurately described within a purely thermodynamic framework.

</details>


### [131] [A Universal Control Budget for First-Passage Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06368)
*Shiling Liang,Ruicheng Bao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了关于有限马尔可夫链中首次通过时间对转移速率变化的对数敏感性的两个普适规律：敏感性绝对值不超过1，且所有速率的敏感性之和为-1，构成守恒的控制预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏对均首通时间如何响应速率变化的一般性约束，希望建立普适的理论框架来理解反应完成时间的调控极限。

Method: 基于有限马尔可夫链模型，分析任意速率变化对平均首次通过时间的对数敏感性，并推导其上下界及求和性质。

Result: 发现每个速率对平均首通时间的对数敏感性绝对值不超过1，且所有速率的敏感性总和恒为-1；多速率联合变化（如势垒或势阱改变）对完成时间的影响仍受限于单个速率的最大可能影响。

Conclusion: 首次通过时间的调控存在守恒的控制预算，解释了动力学校对机制在识别效率上的理论上限及其对底物浓度的敏感性代价。

Abstract: The first-passage time is the natural observable of reaction completion, yet how its mean responds to a rate change has lacked a general constraint. We show that the logarithmic sensitivity of the mean first-passage time of any finite Markov chain to any rate is bounded by one in magnitude, and that these sensitivities sum to -1. Together the two laws form a conserved control budget: speeding completion through some transitions must be paid for by others, and a coordinated change shifts the completion time only as far as the budget allows. Raising an activation barrier or shifting the depth of a well moves many rates at once, yet neither can shift the completion time further than a single rate could. The budget caps kinetic-proofreading discrimination at the checkpoint count, and prices it in sensitivity to substrate concentration.

</details>
