### [132] [Improving the efficiency of infectious disease prevention trials using negative control outcome event times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05261)
*Ethan Ashby,Zhewei Zhang,Tanya P. Garcia,Ting Ye,Holly Janes,Bo Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过调整负控制结果（NCO）事件时间来提高传染病预防干预随机试验中治疗效应估计精度的新方法，该方法在HVTN 704/HPTN 085试验中显著降低了估计方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传染病预防干预的随机试验中，个体对病原体的暴露是主要预后因素，但通常在基线时无法测量，导致传统协变量调整增益有限。

Method: 提出调整负控制结果（NCO）事件时间的方法，并形式化其有效性假设，推导出当主要结果和NCO事件时间均被右删失时的高效影响函数，构建交叉拟合的一阶估计量。

Result: 数值实验显示，当NCO事件时间无信息时，所提估计量与基准方法相当；当NCO事件时间更具预后性时，精度提高。应用于HVTN 704/HPTN 085试验时，相较于基线协变量调整，调整细菌性性传播感染的时间使预防效果估计的方差减少约27%。

Conclusion: 调整负控制结果事件时间是一种有效提升传染病预防试验中估计精度的方法，尤其在NCO事件时间具有较强预后性时表现优异。

Abstract: Baseline covariate adjustment can enhance the efficiency of randomized trials by improving precision of treatment effect estimates. However, the precision gain depends on how strongly the baseline covariates are prognostic for the primary outcome. In randomized trials of infectious disease prevention interventions (e.g., vaccines or passively administered antibodies), an individual's exposure to the pathogen is a leading prognostic factor but is rarely measurable at baseline. Hence, conventional covariate adjustment offers limited precision gain in prevention trials. We propose adjusting for a negative control outcome (NCO) event time, which is causally unaffected by the intervention but shares overlapping exposure mechanisms with the primary outcome. We formalize assumptions under which adjustment for the NCO event time is valid, and show that right-censoring of the NCO event time further complicates adjustment. We derive the efficient influence function for the treatment-arm-specific survivor function of the primary outcome when both the primary outcome and the NCO event time are right-censored, and use it to construct a cross-fitted, one-step estimator that is multiply robust to nuisance misspecification and asymptotically efficient when the nuisances are estimated accurately. In numerical experiments, our estimator compares comparably to benchmarks when the NCO event time is uninformative, and gains precision as the NCO event time is more prognostic for the primary outcome. We apply our method to HVTN 704/HPTN 085, a randomized, double-blinded trial of VRC01, a broadly neutralizing antibody against HIV-1. Adjusting for the time to a bacterial sexually transmitted infection --- a negative control outcome for HIV-1 acquisition --- reduced the estimated variance of the prevention efficacy estimate by approximately 27%, compared to roughly 2.5% for baseline covariate adjustment.

</details>


### [133] [Precision and Decisiveness as Goals: Reliable Sequential Hypothesis Testing with a Dual Stopping Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05301)
*Eyal A. Kazin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的贝叶斯自适应方法“决策性精确为目标”（DPitG），用于序列假设检验，通过同时满足精度目标和明确结论来减少不明确结果并避免假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的HDI+ROPE方法因将停止规则与决策标准耦合可能导致早期假阳性；而完全解耦的PitG方法虽无偏但常产生不明确结果。需要一种兼顾可靠性和效率的方法。

Method: 提出DPitG方法，要求后验最高密度区间（HDI）宽度达到预设精度目标ω，并且HDI完全落在ROPE内或外以得出明确结论。提供样本量规划公式N∝V/ω²、在线计算器和开源代码。

Result: 在公平硬币模拟中（ω=0.08, ROPE=0.5±0.05），DPitG将PitG的不明确率从62%降至2%，仅多花费5%样本，且无假阳性；在测试的ω范围内，DPitG的明确性始终高于97%。

Conclusion: DPitG是一种完全预设、可预注册的序列检验方法，在保证高结论性的同时消除系统性假阳性，是需要可靠结论时的首选方法。

Abstract: Sequential hypothesis testing offers flexibility over fixed-sample designs, but stopping rules coupled to decision criteria risk confirmation bias through early peeking. The HDI+ROPE algorithm exemplifies this: it stops as soon as the posterior Highest Density Interval (HDI) falls entirely outside a Region of Practical Equivalence (ROPE) around the null (rejecting it) or entirely inside, which enables positive acceptance of the null, something Null Hypothesis Significance Testing cannot do. However, this coupling means a wide HDI can satisfy the criterion on early, unrepresentative samples, incurring a systematic false-positive cost. Fully decoupled methods, such as ``Precision is the Goal'' (PitG), eliminate this bias by halting only once a target HDI width $ω$ is reached; yet, by divorcing the stopping rule from the decision criterion, PitG frequently yields inconclusive outcomes, particularly when the null hypothesis is true. We propose ``Decisive Precision is the Goal'' (DPitG), which requires both the precision target and a conclusive verdict to be satisfied simultaneously. In fair coin simulations ($ω=0.08$, ROPE$=0.5\pm0.05$), DPitG reduces the PitG inconclusive rate from 62% to 2% at a median cost of only 5% more samples, with zero false positives; HDI+ROPE achieves comparable conclusiveness only at the cost of false positives. Across the $ω$ range tested, DPitG conclusiveness remains above 97% while PitG's varies widely. We provide a closed-form planning formula $N\propto V/ω^2$ (where $V$ is the observation variance), an online interactive calculator, and open-source code. Demonstrated on single-group binary data, the framework extends readily to continuous outcomes and two-group comparisons. DPitG's fully pre-specified stopping rule is compatible with pre-registration standards and is the method of choice whenever a reliable verdict is required.

</details>


### [134] [Joint Spatial and Temporal Generalized Dissimilarity Mixed Modeling (stGDMM) for Beta Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05352)
*Philip A. White,Henry A. Frye,Jasper A. Slingsby,John A. Silander,,Hana Petersen,Alan E. Gelfand*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文扩展了空间广义差异混合模型（spGDMM），引入时间动态，以更丰富地建模时空变化下的β多样性，并在南非开普植物区数据集上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决传统广义差异模型在时空动态分析中的局限性，特别是缺乏对时间变化的建模能力，本文旨在发展一个能够同时捕捉空间和时间动态的统计框架。

Method: 提出扩展的空间广义差异混合模型（spGDMM），将β多样性建模从纯空间扩展到时空联合建模，使用Bray-Curtis等相异性度量，并在乘积空间上进行统计推断。

Result: 模型揭示了β多样性在时间和空间上的复杂动态，提供了比传统GDM更丰富的建模能力，并成功应用于南非开普植物区的实证数据。

Conclusion: 扩展的spGDMM为生物多样性监测提供了更强有力的统计工具，特别适用于分析长期生态变化和保护规划。

Abstract: Generalized dissimilarity models (GDMs) have emerged as a valuable tool for formal statistical analysis of biodiversity. In particular, beta diversity, measured using dissimilarity measures, e.g., the Bray-Curtis dissimilarity in our case, provides a statistical summary of the difference in species composition between sites. It also provides novel data for spatial and spatio-temporal modeling as it resides over the product space of one space-time pair and a second space-time pair. In earlier work we developed the spatial generalized dissimilarity mixed model (spGDMM) to remedy some of the stochastic issues concerned with the foundational GDM in the literature. Here, we extend that work to include dynamics. We find much richer modeling opportunities as we consider beta diversity with regard to change in time as well as space. We illustrate with a dataset from the Cape Floristic Region (CFR) in South Africa.

</details>


### [135] [Sparse Principal Component Analysis via Wavelets for Distributed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05386)
*Giovanni Barbosa Herrero,Rodney Vasconcelos Fonseca,Aluísio Pinheiro*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Johnstone & Lu (2009)的小波稀疏化方法与Fan et al. (2019)的分布式PCA框架的新方法，旨在在高维场景下降低通信成本的同时保持特征子空间估计质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于数据量大和隐私问题，联邦学习中的分布式数据处理成为挑战，尤其是在高维情况下传统PCA不一致的问题需要解决。

Method: 将小波基稀疏化和变量选择引入到分布式的主成分分析中，在不访问完整数据样本的情况下估计全局特征子空间，并减少通信开销。

Result: 在维度d≥152（λ=25）或d≥252（λ=50）时，该方法在估计误差上优于Fan et al. (2019)，且在整个测试范围内传输的系数更少。

Conclusion: 所提方法在高维分布式环境中能有效提升PCA估计的一致性并显著降低通信成本。

Abstract: The large volume of data and concerns about data privacy have motivated the development of techniques for distributed data, a problem also known as federated learning. In this scenario, sub-samples of the data are divided across different machines, and statistics must be computed over that data without direct access to the full sample. Johnstone & Lu (2009, JASA) show that principal component analysis (PCA) is statistically inconsistent in the high-dimensional regime, and propose a way to recover consistency through wavelet-based sparsification and variable selection. Fan et al. (2019, AoS) show a way to perform this same estimation -- specifically, to estimate the eigenspace that would be obtained if all the data were pooled together, even though it remains effectively distributed -- without addressing the high-dimensional regime. This work incorporates the wavelet-based sparsification of Johnstone & Lu (2009) into the distributed PCA framework of Fan et al. (2019), aiming to reduce communication cost without compromising the quality of the eigenspace estimation. Simulations across $d \in [52, 5000]$ show that the proposed method overtakes Fan et al. (2019) in estimation error beyond a clear dimensional threshold ($d \geq 152$ for $λ=25$, $d \geq 252$ for $λ=50$), while transmitting systematically fewer coefficients throughout the entire range studied. This study was financed by the Sao Paulo Research Foundation (FAPESP), Brazil. Process Number #2023/02538-0 and Number #2025/21250-2.

</details>


### [136] [Inference for subgraph densities in noisy dynamic networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05407)
*Peter W. MacDonald,Eric D. Kolaczyk*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于估计和推断时间序列动态网络中子图密度的统计方法，能够调整网络边的观测误差，并具有良好的渐近性质。通过构建隐马尔可夫网络模型，解决了非独立网络重复观测与多时点网络快照有效聚合的问题，支持对多个时间点的子图密度进行联合推断，从而实现对动态网络的正式统计比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有方法在处理非独立网络重复观测和多时点网络快照聚合方面的不足，本文旨在发展一种鲁棒且高效的统计方法，以应对现实场景中常见的动态、含噪网络数据。

Method: 提出基于隐马尔可夫网络模型的统计框架，对动态网络序列建模，显式调整边的观测误差，并利用渐近理论保证估计的一致性与有效性，同时扩展至多时间点的联合推断。

Result: 所提方法在大规模网络下具有良好的理论性质，能有效处理含噪、非独立的网络重复数据，实现对动态子图密度的稳健估计与多时点统计比较。

Conclusion: 该方法显著拓展了对噪声动态网络的分析能力，为实际中广泛存在的时序网络数据提供了强有力的统计工具。

Abstract: In this work we develop statistical methodology to estimate and perform inference on subgraph densities using time-indexed, or dynamic network sequences. These estimates explicitly adjust for observation errors for the network edges, and have good theoretical properties as the size of the network grows. By specifying a stochastically evolving hidden Markov network model, we address two important directions for further investigation identified by Chang et al. (2022): robustness to non-identical network replicates, and efficient aggregation of multiple available network snapshots. These new methods vastly expand the analysis of noisy networks to new data settings, as network replicates are commonly observed dynamically. The methodology is also extended to consider joint inference for subgraph densities at multiple time points, to facilitate formal statistical comparison of dynamic network snapshots.

</details>


### [137] [Risk-Aware Quantile Learning for Personalized Dynamic Treatment Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05434)
*Chunyin Lei,Annie Qu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RQDTR的风险感知分位数动态治疗策略框架，用于在多治疗选择下优化临床结局的特定分位数，同时显式整合治疗相关风险，并通过角度建模实现多类别治疗决策规则的联合学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分位数动态治疗策略方法主要局限于仅考虑疗效且处理二元治疗，难以满足临床中对风险控制和多治疗选择的需求，因此需要一种更全面、兼顾疗效与风险的决策框架。

Method: 提出RQDTR框架，优化累积潜在结果的预设分位数，引入治疗相关风险；设计基于角度的建模方法以联合学习多类别治疗决策规则；构建三种可解释子类：仅疗效分位数学习、带群体风险控制的约束学习和基于效用的复合收益-风险学习。

Result: 理论分析证明了识别性、oracle等价性、Fisher一致性、估计策略的一致性及有限样本误差界；在模拟研究及All of Us抑郁症和MIMIC-III脓毒症数据中的应用表明，RQDTR优于现有方法，能改善尾部疗效并实现更优的收益-风险权衡。

Conclusion: RQDTR是一种灵活且可解释的框架，能够有效支持多治疗选择下的风险感知、分布尾部优化的临床决策，具有良好的理论保证和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Sequential clinical decision-making often involves more than maximizing average efficacy. Clinicians may need to simultaneously optimize clinically relevant tails of the outcome distribution, control treatment-related risk, and choose among multiple treatment options. Existing quantile dynamic treatment regime (DTR) methods capture distributional features of treatment outcomes but remain largely restricted to efficacy-only objectives and binary treatments. To address these limitations, we propose Risk-Aware Quantile Dynamic Treatment Regimes (RQDTR), a unified framework that optimizes a prespecified quantile of the cumulative potential outcome while explicitly incorporating treatment-related risk. We also develop an angle-based formulation for jointly learning decision rules across multiple treatment categories. Our framework includes three interpretable subclasses: efficacy-only quantile learning, constraint-based learning with population-level risk control, and utility-based learning through a composite benefit-risk utility. Theoretically, we establish identification and oracle equivalence, Fisher consistency of the smoothed surrogate, consistency of the estimated regime, and finite-sample performance error rates. Extensive simulation studies and applications to All of Us major depressive disorder and MIMIC-III sepsis data demonstrate that RQDTR improves tail-oriented efficacy and achieves more favorable benefit-risk trade-offs than existing quantile DTR methods.

</details>


### [138] [Fast Power Evaluation under Biased-Coin Minimization: Sampling and Randomization Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05527)
*Masahiro Kojima*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可重用的层间不平衡高斯近似（SIGA）框架，用于在偏倚硬币最小化设计中高效进行样本量和功效计算，通过分解固定得分统计量并引入两种校准方法（SIGA-S和SIGA-R），显著降低计算成本，同时保持与传统重随机化检验相近的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在采用预设随机化检验的模拟试验中，由于每次模拟都需要重复随机化过程，导致在偏倚硬币最小化设计下的功效和样本量评估计算负担过重，因此需要一种更高效的近似方法。

Method: 通过将固定得分统计量精确分解为联合层间不平衡和正交的层内成分，构建SIGA框架；在明确的分配复制限制条件下，提出基于抽样校准的SIGA-S和基于随机化校准的SIGA-R两种方法，分别用于估计边际均值或风险差异边界处的方差，并利用配对路径协方差刻画条件方差差异。

Result: 综合基准测试表明，SIGA-S和SIGA-R与对应的参考随机化检验在功效上高度一致；基于真实试验设计的模拟显示两者功率相近，且计算效率显著优于嵌套重随机化方法。

Conclusion: SIGA框架提供了一种高效、可重用的近似方案，能够在保证统计性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，适用于复杂随机化设计下的设计阶段统计推断。

Abstract: Design-stage power and sample-size evaluation under biased-coin minimization can be computationally intensive when a prespecified randomization test is reproduced within every simulated trial. We develop a reusable stratum-imbalance Gaussian approximation (SIGA) framework by exactly decomposing a fixed-score statistic into joint-stratum imbalance and orthogonal within-stratum components. Under explicit allocation-copy limit conditions for the same absolute-imbalance rule, the sampling-calibrated procedure, SIGA-S, consistently estimates the repeated-sampling variance at a marginal mean- or risk-difference boundary. At a nonsharp boundary, the conditional variance of a fixed-score randomization test can differ because the score contains the observed allocation path. To characterize this distinction, we express the first-order variance gap as a quadratic form involving pair-path covariance and introduce the randomization-calibrated procedure, SIGA-R, based on a reusable paired allocation-only calibration to approximate the conditional reference distribution. Separate comprehensive benchmarks showed close agreement between each SIGA procedure and the corresponding reference randomization test. A trial-inspired simulation based on published aggregate planning characteristics likewise produced similar power for SIGA-S, SIGA-R and the reference randomization test, while both reusable calibration procedures substantially reduced computation relative to nested rerandomization.

</details>


### [139] [Two base rates, two weights: base-rate neglect has a second axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05658)
*Adam Y. Shavit*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的框架，将基础率忽视分为两种独立的错误：对先验概率校正不足（经典的基础率忽视）和对线索频率校正不足（线索密度效应），并通过贝叶斯方程中的两个权重统一描述。这两种忽视应可分别操纵，形成双重分离，但尚未被实验证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将基础率忽视视为单一错误，忽略了线索本身频率的影响。作者旨在区分这两种不同的基础率校正，并揭示现有模型在实验设计下可能掩盖了它们之间的差异。

Method: 通过构建一个包含两个权重（先验权重和线索频率权重）的贝叶斯模型来统一分析基础率忽视与线索密度效应，并指出在不同任务中（如分级评分与二选一判断）这些权重的表现差异。同时检验该框架能否解释信号检测、因果强度等已有概念。

Result: 发现线索频率权重在分级评分任务中显现，而在二选一任务中被抵消；多个学习记忆模型内部隐含相同的线索频率权重，但在常规实验设计下无法表现出差异；预测两种忽视可实现双重分离。

Conclusion: 基础率忽视应区分为两种独立的认知偏差——对先验和对线索频率的校正不足，二者应能被单独操纵，这一预测构成新框架的核心，尚待实验验证。

Abstract: Base-rate neglect is usually treated as one mistake: giving the prior too little weight. Turning the co-occurrences you see into a useful judgment, though, means correcting for two base rates, not one. The first is the familiar prior, how common the outcome is. The second is how common the cue itself is. Those are two separate mistakes, and a learner can make either one alone. Under-correcting the prior is classical base-rate neglect; under-correcting the cue is the cue-density effect of contingency learning, long studied but not previously recognised as a kind of base-rate neglect. We write both corrections as two weights in one Bayesian equation. The task decides which weight it can measure: the cue-frequency weight appears only in graded ratings, because a two-choice test cancels it. At their extremes the two weights recover familiar quantities: base-rate neglect, the signal-detection criterion, the contiguity/sensitivity/validity triple, and the "lift" measure of causal strength. The same cue-frequency weight also sits inside six standard learning-and-memory models; they seem to agree, but only because the usual experiments squeeze the data into a form where they cannot disagree. Above all, the two neglects should be separately manipulable: an experimenter can move one without moving the other. That is a double dissociation, and a one-parameter account cannot produce it. That prediction is the framework's centre, and it has not yet been tested. This paper lays out the framework and the rating experiment that would settle it; a companion paper fits the two weights to an existing colour-flavour dataset.

</details>


### [140] [Multiparametric MRI Radiomics and Machine Learning Framework for Predicting Treatment Response in Glioblastoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05733)
*Suchibrata Patra*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本研究利用基于动态对比增强MRI的放射组学特征结合MGMT状态，构建个体化药代动力学模型以区分胶质母细胞瘤治疗后的真性进展与假性进展，显著提高了诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在胶质母细胞瘤（GBM）治疗后，真性进展（TP）与假性进展（PsP）在常规影像上表现相似，难以区分，但两者的临床处理截然不同，因此亟需一种可靠的非侵入性鉴别方法。

Method: 研究纳入82例IDH野生型GBM患者，使用五种药代动力学模型对DCE-MRI数据进行体素级拟合，并通过AIC最小化选择最优模型，生成个性化的Ktrans、Ve、Vp和taui图。提取1073个放射组学特征，经Mann-Whitney U检验和Elastic Net降维后，结合MGMT状态训练五种分类器。

Result: 结合MGMT状态的随机森林分类器表现最佳，AUC达0.89，敏感度0.93，特异度0.76，F1分数0.90，优于未结合MGMT（0.84）、T1增强基线（0.72）及单模型Tofts分析（0.68）。Ktrans图的形态和纹理特征及肿瘤体积为最强预测因子，MGMT具独立贡献。

Conclusion: 基于体素级自适应药代动力学模型的放射组学结合MGMT状态可有效提升TP与PsP的无创鉴别能力，具有临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Distinguishing True Progression (TP) from Pseudo-Progression (PsP) after chemoradiotherapy remains a major diagnostic challenge in GBM, as both entities present near-identical appearances on conventional contrast-enhanced post-treatment MRI. This distinction carries substantial clinical weight, since TP and PsP demand divergent management yet cannot be reliably separated on routine imaging alone. We investigated whether radiomic features derived from a parsimonious, voxel-wise pharmacokinetic model of dynamic contrast-enhanced (DCE) MRI, combined with MGMT status, could discriminate between the two. The cohort comprised 82 adults with IDH-wildtype GBM who developed a new contrast-enhancing lesion within six months of chemoradiotherapy; classification (53 TP, 29 PsP) was established by histopathology where available (n=52) and modified RANO criteria otherwise (n=30). At every voxel, contrast-concentration time courses were fitted to five candidate pharmacokinetic models, and the best fit was retained by AIC minimisation, yielding parsimonious Ktrans, Ve, Vp, and taui maps adapting to local heterogeneity rather than a single fixed model across the tumour. Following segmentation, 1,073 radiomic descriptors were extracted and reduced via Mann-Whitney U filtering and Elastic Net, then used to train five classifiers across four feature configurations. A Random Forest classifier combining parsimonious DCE-MRI radiomics with MGMT status achieved the best discrimination (mean AUC 0.89, sensitivity 0.93, specificity 0.76, F1 0.90), outperforming features without MGMT (0.84), a T1-post-contrast baseline (0.72), and a single-model extended-Tofts analysis (0.68). Shape and textural descriptors of the Ktrans map, with tumour volume, were the strongest predictors, MGMT contributing a smaller, independent effect. Allowing the model to vary voxel-wise improves non-invasive discrimination.

</details>


### [141] [Fuzzy network jump models for soft dynamic clustering of graph-structured data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05786)
*Federico P. Cortese*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于基于加权图节点的时间变化观测聚类的模糊网络跳跃模型，通过模拟和实际交通数据验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了对时间变化且与图结构相关的观测数据进行有效聚类，需要一种能够同时处理空间和时间平滑性的方法。

Method: 引入模糊网络跳跃模型，采用具有空间和时间正则化的软聚类框架，并通过利用正则项的二次结构进行高效的交替优化求解。

Result: 模拟研究表明该方法能准确恢复真实的成员概率，并优于现有聚类方法；在旧金山交通网络数据中的应用成功识别出可解释的交通状态及其时空演化。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型在处理图结构化时间序列数据的聚类任务中表现出色，具有良好的解释性和实用性。

Abstract: We introduce a fuzzy network jump model for clustering time-varying observations indexed by the nodes of a weighted graph. The framework allows flexible graph representations with spatial and temporal regularization promoting smooth soft cluster assignments across connected nodes and consecutive time points. Estimation is performed through an efficient alternating optimization scheme that exploits the quadratic structure of the regularization terms. A simulation study covering different levels of spatial dependence and cluster overlap shows that the proposed method accurately recovers the true membership probabilities and outperforms competing clustering methods. An application to traffic-network data for the city of San Francisco identifies interpretable traffic regimes and reveals their evolution over time and across connected road segments.

</details>


### [142] [Joint Multiple Imputation of Node Attributes and Network Ties in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05895)
*Robert W. Krause*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为netimpute的R语言包，用于联合填补社会网络中缺失的节点属性和网络关系，结合网络结构与属性同质性指标，通过迭代算法实现更准确的多重插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺失数据填补方法在处理社会网络数据时，往往无法同时兼顾节点属性和网络结构，且通常将两者分开处理，导致估计偏差。因此需要一个能联合建模并利用网络特征的工具。

Method: 开发netimpute R包，包含计算节点结构性与属性同质性指标、主成分降维、基于MR-QAP思想的二元关系回归，并提出netmice()函数采用链式方程循环填补缺失属性与网络关系；使用逐边吉布斯抽样更新网络关系，实时更新互惠性和共同联系等内生统计量。

Result: netimpute能够有效整合网络结构信息与节点属性进行联合填补，支持结构零值、网络间逻辑约束、社会关系模型随机截距、自定义交互项模型及针对目标变量的网络感知预测变量选择（netquickpred）。

Conclusion: netimpute提供了一套完整且灵活的框架，用于同时处理社会网络中节点属性和连接关系的缺失问题，提升了多重插补在网络数据分析中的适用性与准确性。

Abstract: Missing data in social network studies routinely affects both node-level attributes and the network ties themselves. Standard multiple imputation software such as mice (van Buuren and Groothuis-Oudshoorn, 2011) handles the former well but has no notion of network structure, while dedicated network-imputation procedures typically treat tie imputation and attribute imputation as separate problems. This paper introduces netimpute, an R package that (a) computes a broad battery of node-level structural and attribute homophily measures across one or more networks, (b) can reduce these to a manageable set of principal component predictors while optionally preserving specific raw measures that are themselves part of substantive hypotheses, (c) provides a dyadic (cell-level) regression for network ties in the spirit of MR-QAP (Krackhardt, 1988), and (d) combines both into netmice(), a chained-equations routine that jointly imputes missing node attributes and missing network ties by cycling between them, using network-derived predictors for attributes and attribute-derived (and other-network-derived) predictors for ties. Network ties are updated tie-wise by default: a sequential Gibbs step redraws each missing tie one at a time, conditional on all previously imputed ties, refreshing the endogenous statistics (reciprocity, shared contacts) after every single draw via change statistics. The package additionally supports structural zeros, logical constraints between networks, social-relations-model random intercepts in the tie model, custom imputation models with interactions among any internally created terms, and network-aware per-target predictor selection (netquickpred()). We describe the package's design choices, the measures it computes for binary and non-negative weighted networks, and illustrate its use with a reproducible example

</details>


### [143] [A Regression-Based Framework for the ACF, PACF, Durbin-Levinson Recursion, and One-Step-Ahead Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06334)
*Kellen Gong,Fang Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于回归的统一教学框架，用于解释自相关函数（ACF）、偏自相关函数（PACF）、Durbin-Levinson递推和单步预测，使时间序列分析更贴近学生已掌握的回归知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACF和PACF通常作为计算方法教授，与学生熟悉的回归框架脱节，导致理解困难。本文旨在通过回归视角降低学习时间序列模型的概念门槛。

Method: 将ACF视为对平稳过程与其滞后项进行简单线性回归的系数，PACF视为逐步扩展的多元回归中最新滞后项的系数，并利用普通最小二乘法解释Durbin-Levinson递推。通过MA(1)和AR(1)过程的具体示例展示ACF和PACF的截尾与拖尾特性。

Result: 建立了ACF、PACF与回归之间的直接联系，揭示了传统时间序列工具在回归框架下的直观解释，且该方法能自然导出其典型行为模式。

Conclusion: 该回归为基础的教学框架有助于弥合回归课程与时间序列课程之间的概念鸿沟，提升教学效果，并为课堂提供实用的教学建议。

Abstract: The autocorrelation function (ACF) and partial autocorrelation function (PACF) are foundational tools for identifying autoregressive moving-average (ARMA) models, yet they are often introduced to students as computational recipes disconnected from the regression framework students already know. This note develops a unified, regression-based instructional framework for the ACF, the PACF, the Durbin-Levinson recursion, and one-step-ahead prediction in stationary time series. We show that the ACF is precisely the coefficient from a simple linear regression of a stationary process on one of its own lags, that the PACF is the coefficient on the newest lag in an expanding multiple regression model, and that the Durbin-Levinson recursion is nothing more than a sequence of partial regressions, each expressible using ordinary least-squares identities from a first course in regression. Worked examples for the MA(1) and AR(1) processes illustrate how the tailing-off and cutting-off behavior of the ACF and PACF emerge directly from this viewpoint. We argue that this framing lowers the conceptual barrier between regression courses and time series courses and provide classroom-oriented suggestions for instruction.

</details>

### [144] [QCD Chiral Crossover Line from Lee-Yang Edge Singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05752)
*Heng-Tong Ding,Swagato Mukherjee,Peter Petreczky,Kai-Fan Ye*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于普适性的方法，利用复化学势平面上的李-杨边缘奇点重构QCD手征交叉线，并通过格点QCD数据验证其一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在不依赖小化学势展开的前提下，从李-杨零点提取信息，精确重建QCD手征相变线。

Method: 将格点QCD模拟得到的复平面李-杨零点视为边缘奇点，映射到普适的手征李-杨边缘，结合普适性与手征标度理论确定手征临界线和拟临界交叉线的μ_B依赖关系。

Result: 应用Wuppertal-Budapest合作组的高统计量数据，重建的交叉线曲率与现有格点结果一致，拟合的手征极限转变温度也符合标度分析结果。

Conclusion: 李-杨奇点信息结合普适手征标度可为QCD交叉线提供定量约束，且该框架可随未来更精确的零点数据系统改进。

Abstract: We propose a universality-based reconstruction of the QCD chiral crossover line from Lee-Yang edge singularities in the complex baryon chemical potential plane. The framework maps lattice-extracted complex Lee-Yang-zero estimates, treated as proxies for edge singularities, to the universal chiral Lee-Yang edge and thereby determines the $μ_B$ dependence of both the chiral critical line in the light-quark chiral limit and the pseudo-critical crossover line at physical quark masses. As an illustration, we apply the framework to Lee-Yang-zero estimates recently obtained by the Wuppertal-Budapest collaboration from high-statistics lattice QCD simulations. Without imposing the previously determined small-$μ_B$ expansion of the crossover line as input, the reconstructed curvature is consistent with existing continuum lattice-QCD results at small $μ_B$. The fitted chiral-limit transition temperature is also compatible with existing chiral-scaling analyses. These results demonstrate that lattice information on Lee-Yang singularities, combined with universal chiral scaling, provides a quantitatively consistent constraint on the QCD crossover line within the present temperature window and establishes a framework that can be systematically improved with future Lee-Yang-zero determinations.

</details>


<div id='physics.hist-ph'></div>

# physics.hist-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Newton's Second Law: A Theoretical Identity Derived from the Principle of Excluded Perpetual Motion and the Weak Equivalence Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06266)
*Lars Nordmann*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过排除永动机原理（PEPM）和弱等效原理（WEP），从更深层的物理原则推导出牛顿第二定律，表明其并非力学的基本公理，而是能量守恒与爱因斯坦等效原理的结构性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战牛顿第二定律作为不可约基本公理的传统观点，探索其是否可由更基本的物理原理推导而出。

Method: 结合Suppes的操作测量协议、Stevin的静力学与Galileo的动力学分析，基于PEPM和WEP两个物理原则，对惯性质量和引力质量进行独立定义，并推导出F=ma。

Result: 在PEPM和WEP约束下，牛顿第二定律成为可被推导的理论恒等式；该框架排除了MOND类修改惯性理论的可能性，支持Kibble天平实现千克定义的位置不变性，并统一了扭秤与自由落体实验对WEP的检验。

Conclusion: 牛顿第二定律不是力学的起点，而是更深物理原则（能量守恒与爱因斯坦等效原理）的结构性必然结果。

Abstract: For more than three centuries, Newton's Second Law (F=ma) has governed mechanics with undisputed success, yet its epistemic authority has rested on empirical adequacy alone. That empirical contingency vanishes under two physical principles - the Principle of Excluded Perpetual Motion (PEPM) and the Weak Equivalence Principle (WEP) - from which the law emerges as a structural necessity of admissible mechanics. Under the PEPM constraint, Suppes' operational measurement protocol grounds gravitational mass and force as independent primitives. The fusion of Stevin's static and Galileo's kinematic inclined-plane analyses establishes F/a as a well-defined, object-intrinsic quantity - the operational definition of inertial mass. The WEP compels the equivalence of inertial and gravitational mass without presupposing Newton's Second Law. Substituting this equivalence into the definition of inertial mass yields the law as a theoretical identity between independently defined quantities. The derivation is anchored in gravity because weight uniquely bridges statics and kinematics, yet the result is independent of the underlying force mechanism. The derivation carries structural consequences: modified-inertia formulations of MOND are inadmissible under the PEPM-WEP constraint; the location-invariance of the Kibble-balance realization of the SI kilogram is secured on first principles; and under the PEPM alone, torsion-balance and free-fall experiments constitute equally direct tests of the WEP. More fundamentally, Newton's Second Law is not an irreducible axiom of mechanics, but a structural consequence of deeper physical principles: energy conservation and the Einstein Equivalence Principle.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Discrete energy as an exact label-free training objective for finite-element surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05437)
*Ruifeng Cao,Xidan Song*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了无需参考解的有限元代理模型训练方法，通过离散势能差提供精确训练信号，并证明了其与刚度范数下的监督回归等价。同时给出了误差界、模态收缩性、共轭梯度后处理及JEPA预训练条件分离等性质，所有结论均经数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督训练需要大量参考解，计算成本高。本文旨在寻找一种无需参考解的训练信号，以降低对昂贵求解的依赖。

Method: 利用组装后的离散势能作为训练信号，推导并证明了预测能量与真实解能量之差等于刚度范数误差的一半平方，且能量梯度等于刚度加权误差。结合多种数学工具分析误差界、模态特性及时间离散化影响。

Result: 证明了无标签离散能量最小化与刚度范数下监督回归具有相同唯一极小值和梯度；给出了位移误差的能量间隙上界、模态收缩恒等式、共轭梯度后处理的切比雪夫界，以及JEPA在共享刚度算子上的条件潜在分离命题及其数值反例；所有含数值声明均通过合成问题和16个验证实例双重验证。

Conclusion: 离散势能在线弹性静态问题中可作为精确且有效的无标签训练信号，但该构造不能直接推广到通过作用量泛函最小化的弹动力学问题，需采用特定时间离散格式恢复精确性。

Abstract: Supervised training of finite-element (FE) surrogate models requires reference solutions, and each reference solution is obtained by solving the system that the surrogate is intended to replace. The assembled discrete potential energy provides a training signal that requires no reference solution. This note records, with proofs, the identities that make this signal exact for linear elastostatics: the difference between the energy of a prediction and the energy of the reference solution equals one half of the squared stiffness-norm error, and the gradient of the energy equals the stiffness-weighted error. Label-free discrete-energy minimisation and supervised regression in the stiffness norm therefore have the same unique minimiser and identical gradients at every point. Around this central result, the note states a conditioning lemma that bounds the displacement error by the energy gap, a modewise contraction identity that explains why the Euclidean displacement error is an unsuitable primary metric, the Chebyshev bound that governs conjugate-gradient post-processing of surrogate predictions, and a conditional latent-separation proposition for joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) pretraining on a shared stiffness operator, with an explicit numerical counterexample that delimits its scope. Every claim with numeric content is implemented as an executable falsification check; the checks were executed twice, on synthetic test problems and on a probe set of 16 instances from the validation split of a pre-registered experimental run, and every inequality holds, with the measured tightness reported. A closing section explains why the construction does not extend to elastodynamics through direct minimisation of the action functional, and which time-discrete formulation restores exactness.

</details>


### [147] [Variable-Horizon Workforce Demand Forecasting with an Aggregate Demand Constraint for Construction Workforce Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05551)
*Hanbyeol park,Jaehyeon Heo,Taekhyun Park,Minseong Kim,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种约束保持的残差分配预测方法（CP-RAF），用于解决建筑项目中具有可变预测范围和总量约束的劳动力需求预测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习模型假设输出长度固定且不考虑总量约束，难以满足实际施工中劳动力规划对可变预测范围和总需求一致性的要求。

Method: 将劳动力需求时间序列表示为系数向量，通过检索具有相似时序模式的历史任务，采用相似性加权平均估计剩余工期的需求分布，并根据预设总量按估计轮廓分配需求，同时调整预测范围并保留轮廓特征。

Result: 在实地数据上的实验表明，CP-RAF在中长期固定长度预测中优于八个基线模型，并在可变长度预测下保持较低误差。

Conclusion: CP-RAF通过将操作约束直接融入预测过程，提供了一个适用于实际施工劳动力分配的预测框架。

Abstract: Workforce planning is a recurring operational decision during construction projects that requires accurate forecasts of the future workforce demand for individual tasks. However, in practice, tasks have different completion dates, resulting in variable forecast horizons. In addition, the sum of the predicted daily workforce demands must equal the total workforce allocation specified in advance. Most existing machine-learning (ML)-based forecasting models assume fixed-length outputs and do not explicitly impose an aggregate demand constraint, making them unsuitable for these operational requirements. To address this problem, this study proposes constraint-preserving residual allocation forecasting (CP-RAF). The CP-RAF represents an observed workforce demand time series as a coefficient vector and retrieves completed tasks with similar temporal shapes. Then, it estimates the allocation profile over the remaining task duration using similarity-weight averaging. The predefined remaining workforce demand for each task was distributed according to the estimated profile, and the forecast horizon was adjusted while retaining profile characteristics. This procedure accommodates variable forecast horizons while preserving the aggregate demand constraints. CP-RAF was evaluated using workforce demand field data. The results showed that CP-RAF outperformed eight baseline models in medium- and long-horizon fixed-length forecasting and maintained low forecast errors under variable-length forecasting. By directly incorporating operational constraints into the forecasting procedure, the proposed method provides a framework suitable for workforce allocation in construction practices.

</details>


### [148] [A hybrid s-version isogeometric strategy for dynamic crack propagation in 2D and 3D problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05589)
*Tianyu He,Kosei Kurosaki,Naoki Morita,Naoto Mitsume,Kazuki Shibanuma*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合s版本的等几何分析方法（hS-IGA），用于动态裂纹扩展分析中近裂纹断裂量的高效精确评估，显著减少了耦合积分点数量并保持了高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统拉格朗日s方法在全局-局部耦合积分中的连续性瓶颈，并保留裂纹表示和动态应力强度因子等后处理所需的拉格朗日局部网格。

Method: 在全局离散中引入B样条基函数，同时在裂纹域保留基于拉格朗日的局部网格，构建混合s-IGA公式，实现标准高斯积分下的精确耦合计算。

Result: 该方法在二维和三维静态及动态裂纹问题中均表现出高精度，相较于传统s方法，耦合积分点分别减少了约81%和95.6%，并准确评估了动态应力强度因子和局部应力。

Conclusion: 所提出的hS-IGA框架为需要可靠评估近裂纹断裂量的动态裂纹扩展分析提供了一种准确且高效的全局-局部策略。

Abstract: A hybrid s-version of isogeometric analysis (hS-IGA) strategy is proposed for accurate and efficient evaluation of near-crack fracture quantities in dynamic crack propagation analysis. The strategy retains the global-local superposition framework of the conventional s-method, while introducing B-spline basis functions only into the global discretisation and preserving a Lagrange-based local mesh in the crack domain. This hybrid formulation is motivated by the continuity-related bottleneck in the global-local coupling integration of the conventional Lagrange-based s-method, and by the need to retain a Lagrange-based local mesh for crack representation and post-processing of the dynamic stress intensity factor (DSIF) and local stress. The resulting formulation removes discontinuities in the coupling integrands caused by the global approximation and enables accurate coupling integration by standard Gauss quadrature without recursive subdivision. The proposed strategy is verified using two-dimensional stationary and dynamic straight-crack problems against the standard finite element method and the conventional s-method, and is further assessed using three-dimensional stationary and dynamically propagating circular-crack problems against the conventional s-method. Results show that the proposed hS-IGA strategy accurately evaluates the DSIF and local stress while retaining the global-local modelling advantages of the s-method. It also substantially reduces the number of integration points required for coupling integration, by approximately 81% in the two-dimensional dynamic benchmark and 95.6% in the three-dimensional dynamic benchmark relative to the conventional s-method. These results demonstrate that the proposed hS-IGA framework provides an accurate and efficient global-local strategy for dynamic crack propagation analyses requiring reliable evaluation of near-crack fracture quantities.

</details>


### [149] [A Label-Free Physics-to-Data Acceleration Framework for Parametric Time-Dependent PDEs with Latent-Space Differential-Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05554)
*Hongjiang Wang,Weizhe Wang,Yingzheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出PHD-SF框架，通过结合SD-TPD分离策略与微分算子学习，在无需全字段标签的情况下实现时间依赖参数PDE的高效跨参数求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习加速方法在物理一致性与计算效率之间存在割裂，且依赖大量高保真数据和显式标签传递，限制了模型泛化性与实用性。

Method: 设计统一的DON架构，包含PIDON、HIDON和DIDON三种模式，采用SD-TPD分离策略，实现空间特征、潜在动力学特征和空间微分算子的生成、继承与增强，支持无标签的谱系建模。

Result: 在四个时变参数PDE基准上验证，仅需一到两个参数样本训练初始物理驱动模型，即可实现准确的跨参数预测与直接推理，端到端成本低于单个PINN训练成本。

Conclusion: PHD-SF提供了一种低数据依赖、低成本、可重用的跨参数PDE求解框架，为多查询参数问题提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient solution of time-dependent parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) is central to computational science and engineering. Existing deep-learning-based accelerators span a physics-to-data spectrum, from physics-informed solvers with strong physical consistency but high computational cost to data-driven surrogates with efficient inference but strong dependence on high-fidelity datasets. These methods have largely evolved in isolation and are often connected only through explicit solution-field labels. We propose PHD-SF, a compact physics-to-data spectrum framework for accelerating time-dependent parametric PDEs. By combining an SD-TPD separation strategy with differential-operator learning, PHD-SF allows reusable model information, including spatial features, latent dynamical features, and spatial differential operators, to be generated, inherited, and enriched across three operating modes, PIDON, HIDON, and DIDON, within a unified DON-based architecture. This design enables label-free spectrum modeling without precomputed full-field solution labels or explicit label transfer among modes. PHD-SF further establishes a separated-solving, hyper-reduction-like enrichment, and direct-inference acceleration path, reducing the dependence on large-scale high-fidelity data generation and its offline cost. Results on four benchmark time-dependent PDEs show accurate cross-parameter solution and direct inference using only one or two representative parameter cases in the initial physics-informed stage. The total end-to-end cost is lower than that required to train a conventional PINN for a single parameter case, while supporting reusable cross-parameter inference. PHD-SF therefore provides an efficient solution path for many-query parametric PDEs.

</details>


### [150] [Quantitative Analysis of Media Bias and Stock Price Dynamics: The 2020 Shock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05899)
*Shivansh Verma,Soham Tulsyan,Sashwat Dhanuka,Anirban Sen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在新冠疫情冲击下，财经新闻与个股股价之间的动态关系，发现整体市场层面的媒体立场与股价关系变化不显著，但部分公司在其结构性断点附近表现出显著的动态关联，强调需进行公司特异性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨财经新闻是影响股价还是仅仅反映已有的市场信息，尤其是在重大外部冲击（如新冠疫情）背景下，现有研究多基于汇总情绪指标，缺乏对个体公司层面的深入分析。

Method: 使用2015至2025年间涵盖26家美国大型企业的628万条新闻标题，筛选出具有实质相关性的企业特定报道，构建每日立场指标，并采用面板回归和带有数据驱动结构性断点的向量自回归模型，分析疫情前后新闻立场与股票收益的关系变化。

Result: 未发现疫情后市场整体层面媒体立场或股价有持续性变化，但在个别公司的结构性断点附近出现了显著的动态关系。

Conclusion: 媒体与市场的互动关系具有企业特异性，理解新闻与价格的互动需要结合具体公司特征，并提出了在变化市场条件下研究该问题的分析框架。

Abstract: Whether financial news influences stock prices or simply reflects information already incorporated into them remains an open question in financial economics. The COVID-19 pandemic provides an opportunity to revisit this question, as it disrupted both news coverage and financial markets on an unprecedented scale. Existing studies have largely approached the problem through aggregate sentiment measures, leaving it unclear whether the observed relationships also hold at the level of individual firms. We study this question using 6.28 million news headlines covering 26 large United States firms between 2015 and 2025. After filtering the corpus to retain materially relevant firm-specific coverage, we construct daily stance measures and examine how their relationship with stock returns changed around the 2020 shock using panel regressions and vector autoregressions with data-driven structural breaks. Our findings indicate that the relationship between financial news and equity markets is more nuanced than aggregate analyses alone suggest. While we find little evidence of a persistent market-wide change in media stance or stock returns following the pandemic, dynamic relationships emerge for a subset of firms around their own structural breaks. Taken together, these results suggest that understanding media-market interactions requires firm specific analysis and provide a framework for studying how news and prices interact under changing market conditions.

</details>


### [151] [Benefits of Shifting Passenger Traffic from Air to Rail: A Case Study of California High-Speed Rail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05636)
*Kaijing Ding,Lu Dai,Mark Hansen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究通过减少航班延误成本的视角，量化了将客运从航空转向高速铁路的好处，预计到2033年可节省2.35亿至3.92亿美元的延误成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估高铁对缓解机场拥堵和航班延误的影响，研究旨在量化航空客流向高速铁路转移所带来的经济效益。

Method: 基于加州高铁2020年商业计划中的 ridership 预测，估算机场间航班减少数量，并将其分配到15分钟时段；采用Lasso模型分析SFO、LAX和SAN机场排队延误减少对全国29个核心机场到达延误的影响，并结合飞机运营成本与乘客时间价值进行货币化评估。

Result: 在2029年预计节省5100万至8800万美元（2018年币值），在2033年预计节省2.35亿至3.92亿美元（2018年币值）的航班延误成本。

Conclusion: 高铁的发展不仅能提供高效出行方式，还能显著降低航空系统延误成本，提升整体交通网络效率。

Abstract: This study provides a method to quantify the benefits of shifting passenger traffic from air to high-speed rail from the perspective of flight-delay cost reduction. We first estimate the number of flight reductions for airport origin-destination pairs based on the high-speed rail ridership forecasts provided in the California High-Speed Rail 2020 Business Plan, and then distribute these flight reductions to quarter-hour intervals. Lasso models are applied to estimate the impact of reduced queuing delays at SFO, LAX, and SAN on arrival delays at the national Core 29 airports. These delay reductions are then monetized using aircraft operating costs and the value of passenger time. We evaluate alternative airport-capacity and flight-schedule scenarios, as well as multiple percentiles of probabilistic high-speed rail ridership forecasts. The resulting estimates indicate flight-delay cost savings of $51-88 million in 2018 dollars in 2029 and $235-392 million in 2018 dollars in 2033.

</details>


### [152] [Hyperelastic Membranes with Implicitly Defined, Continuously Embedded Fibers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05717)
*Michael Wolfgang Kaiser,Thomas-Peter Fries*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于各向异性、超弹性、曲面薄膜的新型力学模型及相应的有限元方法，结合隐式纤维几何描述与膜力学，实现了高阶收敛精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为生物组织和纺织品等材料的简化模型中常见的各向异性超弹性曲面膜提供更精确的力学建模方法。

Method: 基于连续介质力学第一性原理（有限应变理论）建立非线性力学模型，采用切向微分 calculus 实现坐标无关的微分算子，并通过水平集函数隐式描述纤维几何，将其与膜力学耦合；数值上采用表面有限元与虚拟域方法相结合的混合有限元方案。

Result: 在光滑物理场情况下获得了高阶收敛率，验证了数值方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型和数值方法能够有效处理具有隐式纤维结构的各向异性超弹性曲面膜问题，具备良好的收敛性能和应用潜力。

Abstract: A novel mechanical model and corresponding finite element method for anisotropic, hyperelastic, curved membranes are proposed. Hyperelastic fibers are embedded into the otherwise isotropic membrane, being relevant, for example, in reduced models for biological tissues and textiles. The geometrically nonlinear mechanics is formulated based on first principles of continuum mechanics (finite strain theory). The employed differential operators are formulated in a coordinate-free manner, through a framework known as tangential differential calculus. This enables a (semi-)implicit description of the fiber geometry through the intersection of level sets of some scalar function with the explicitly defined membrane surface. The mechanical model of the implicit fibers is then coupled to the mechanics of the membrane. For the numerical analysis, finite elements are applied such that the resulting scheme is a hybrid between classical Surface FEM and fictitious domain methods. For smooth physical fields, higher-order convergence rates are obtained and confirm the success of the numerical method.

</details>


### [153] [Accurate simulation of delamination with a resin-rich layer-dependent penalty stiffness based on structural cohesive elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05881)
*Xiaopeng Ai,Christos Kassapoglou,Boyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于树脂富集层的高阶结构内聚单元，通过区分法向和剪切罚刚度并引入等效单层框架，显著提高了裂纹尖端压缩分布和分层扩展预测的精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 壳型内聚单元因罚刚度假设导致裂纹尖端前方压缩被高估，需提升模拟精度和计算效率。

Method: 在已有高阶结构内聚元基础上，引入树脂富集层依赖的罚刚度，区分法向与剪切刚度，并基于梁理论推导的厚度方向应力分布，将罚刚度从层间扩展到等效单层框架。

Result: 在I型、II型、混合模式及增强型DCB配置的基准问题中验证表明，新方法相比传统方法具有更优的网格收敛性，能更准确预测裂纹尖端的压缩分布和分层扩展行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的改进方法有效提升了内聚单元在复合材料分层模拟中的精度和效率，适用于多种加载模式下的断裂分析。

Abstract: Shell-based cohesive elements tend to overestimate the compression ahead of the crack tip because of approximations in the penalty stiffness. In this study, the higher-order structural cohesive element previously developed by the authors is enhanced with a resin-rich layer-dependent penalty stiffness to improve both computational efficiency and predictive accuracy. The proposed formulation distinguishes between the normal and shear penalty stiffnesses. It extends the resin-rich layer-based penalty stiffness from the layer-wise to the equivalent single-layer framework. This extension is achieved using the through-thickness distributions of the out-of-plane normal and transverse shear stresses derived from beam theory. The proposed method is verified and validated against benchmark problems for Mode I, Mode II, mixed-mode, and reinforced DCB configurations. Compared with the conventional formulation, it exhibits significantly improved mesh convergence and provides more accurate predictions of the compression distribution ahead of the crack tip and the delamination propagation.

</details>


### [154] [Local-Global Feature Mixer and Trend-Guided Consistent Learning for Remaining Useful Life Prediction of Rotating Machinery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05925)
*Hanbyeol Park,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种局部-全局特征混合器（LGFM）和趋势引导的滚动一致性（TG-RC）损失函数，用于改进退化过程建模中的长期健康指标预测稳定性与剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络在递归健康指标预测中易产生误差累积，且难以保持长期退化趋势的一致性，尤其在训练与推理模式不一致时表现不佳。

Method: 设计了LGFM模块以融合原始健康指标序列与统计及退化相关特征，捕捉局部变化与全局状态；并提出TG-RC损失函数，结合多步滚动预测损失与基于全局趋势先验的动态时间规整对齐项，缓解误差累积并引导预测方向。

Result: 在两个公开轴承数据集上验证了该方法，结果显示其显著提升了长期健康指标外推的稳定性和RUL预测精度，同时LGFM保持低计算复杂度，TG-RC损失可通用化集成到其他退化模型中。

Conclusion: 所提LGFM和TG-RC损失有效改善了退化建模中的长期预测一致性与鲁棒性，具有良好的应用潜力与模型兼容性。

Abstract: Degradation process (DP) modeling is widely used for remaining useful life (RUL) prediction, particularly when run-to-failure data are limited. Neural networks can present complex degradation trajectories without prescribing a fixed degradation function; however, recursive health indicator (HI) forecasting is prone to error accumulation and may fail to preserve the irreversible degradation trend over long horizons. To address these limitations, this study proposes a local-global feature mixer (LGFM) and trend-guided rollout-consistent (TG-RC) loss. The LGFM combines the original HI sequence with statistical and degradation-related features to reduce sensitivity to high-frequency noise and capture both local changes and global degradation states. The TG-RC loss supplements the conventional one-step mean squared error with a recursive multi-step rollout loss and a soft dynamic time warping alignment term based on a global trend prior. Consequently, it reduces the discrepancy between training and recursive inference, while guiding the predicted trajectory toward a consistent degradation direction. Experiments on two public bearing datasets show that the proposed framework improves long-term HI extrapolation stability and RUL prediction performance across different inspection times. The LGFM also maintains low computational complexity, while the TG-RC loss can be incorporated into various DP models to improve their long-term forecasting performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [A Quantum Circuit Framework for Protein Ensemble-Level Energetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05491)
*Pratik Patil,Bhushan Bonde,Bhaskar Choubey*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于残基水平的门型量子电路框架，用于粗粒化蛋白质热力学，能够表征异质自由能景观并揭示残基层面的稳定性、耦合关系及信息增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子动力学计算采样成本高，而多数量子方法仅关注单一最优结构，无法探索蛋白质全系综的能量异质性。因此需要一种能有效刻画蛋白质复杂自由能景观的新方法。

Method: 将每个氨基酸表示为两态量子比特（稳定与激发溶剂化状态），基于残基溶剂化能量；通过结构指导的纠缠模块使用参数化受控门编码共价和非共价相互作用，构建残基相互作用网络；通过对电路进行约10^6次测量，获得二进制热力学微观状态，用于计算能量分布、残基耦合、敏感性和信息增益。

Result: 在Trp-cage小蛋白1L2Y和9GDL上验证了该框架，成功再现了类似折叠漏斗的能量分布；比较分析揭示了全局能量分布和残基稳定性谱的变化；耦合与信息理论分析定位了与系综重组相关的残基，多体耦合分析显示电路可分辨直接与间接统计相关性。

Conclusion: 该框架将量子蛋白质建模从单结构优化扩展到系综水平表征，有助于理解粗糙能量景观的关键特征，并可用于指导蛋白质设计、突变图谱绘制和变构通路识别。

Abstract: Proteins occupy heterogeneous free-energy landscapes in which high-entropy ensembles converge toward compact, low-energy basins with multiple sub-states. Molecular dynamics can access these landscapes at atomic resolution, but exhaustive sampling remains computationally demanding. Meanwhile, most quantum approaches target only single optimal structures, leaving full ensemble energetic heterogeneity unexplored. We introduce a residue-level, gate-based quantum circuit framework for coarse-graining protein thermodynamics. Each amino acid is represented as a two-state qubit (stabilised vs. excited solvation state) based on residue solvation energetics. A structure-informed entanglement block then encodes covalent and non-covalent contacts using parameterised controlled gates, embedding correlations across the residue-interaction network. Sampling the circuit ($\sim 10^6$ measurements) yields binary thermodynamic microstates used to compute protein energy distributions, residue-level statistical couplings, energetic sensitivities, and information gains relative to total free energy. We showcase the framework on the benchmark Trp-cage miniprotein 1L2Y (TC5b) and 9GDL, a disulfide-stabilised Trp-cage-fortified exenatide chimera. For 1L2Y, the circuit reproduces a structured, folding-funnel-like energy distribution. Comparative analysis with 9GDL reveals shifts in global energy distributions and residue-level stability profiles. Coupling and information-theoretic analyses localise residues associated with ensemble reorganisation, while multi-body couplings show the circuit resolves both direct and indirect statistical correlations. This framework expands quantum protein modelling beyond single-structure optimisation toward ensemble-level characterisation, capturing key features of rugged energy landscapes to guide protein design, mutation mapping, and allosteric pathway identification.

</details>


### [156] [Viveka: Context-Aware Sensing for Energy Efficiency in Smart Wearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05572)
*Nikhil Sreekumar,Abhishek Chandra*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、上下文感知的框架Viveka，用于在多传感器物联网系统中联合优化传感器选择和采样率，以最小化能耗并保持分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在传感器选择或自适应采样方面存在孤立处理、计算开销大、缺乏上下文细粒度或未考虑误识别上下文带来的风险等问题，难以在节能的同时保证应用可靠性。

Method: 将联合传感器与采样率选择建模为NP难的能量最小化问题；设计Viveka框架，包含一个低成本常驻控制器用于估计上下文及其置信度，并采用基于稳定性与置信度的门控策略，在上下文确定时应用激进的上下文特定配置，否则安全回退；通过特征重要性排列和谱能量分析生成各上下文下的配置。

Result: 在MHEALTH和PAMAP2数据集上的实验表明，Viveka在最佳配置下相比基线可实现最高75%的节能和78%的数据量减少，同时分类准确率仅下降3-5%。

Conclusion: Viveka通过结合上下文置信度评估与安全回退机制，实现了高效能、低风险的多传感器管理，在节能与准确性之间取得了良好平衡，适用于资源受限的物联网系统。

Abstract: The proliferation of multi-sensor Internet of Things (IoT) systems, from Body Sensor Networks (BSNs) to industrial monitoring, is increasingly constrained by strict energy budgets and limited on-device storage. Continuous high-fidelity sensing leads to rapid battery depletion and data gaps that compromise application reliability. Existing strategies address this through sensor selection or adaptive sampling in isolation, or rely on computationally expensive agents for joint optimization. They lack context granularity or introduce significant overhead, and critically, they do not account for the risk that an aggressive, context-specific sensing policy applied to a misidentified context degrades accuracy. In this paper, we formulate joint sensor and sampling-rate selection as an NP-hard energy-minimization problem and propose Viveka, a lightweight, context-aware framework. Viveka couples a cheap, always-on controller that estimates context and how much to trust that estimate with a stability and confidence gated policy that applies an aggressive per-context configuration only when context is certain, and falls back safely otherwise. Per-context configurations are instantiated using permutation feature importance and spectral energy analysis. Evaluation on the MHEALTH and PAMAP2 datasets shows that Viveka achieves up to 75% energy savings and 78% data reduction over standard baselines in a best-case configuration, while maintaining classification accuracy within 3-5% of the baselines.

</details>


### [157] [LC-Implicit-QAOA: Active-Workspace-Capped Exact Objective-and-Gradient Evaluation for Training over Bounded QUBO Light Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05610)
*Chih-Chung Hsu*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: LC-Implicit-QAOA是一种针对QAOA训练中计算瓶颈的高效算法，通过利用因果锥结构和伴随微分技术，在有限资源下实现局部振幅和工作区的优化分配，避免全局状态和成本表的存储，显著提升计算效率与内存控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA训练过程中频繁查询目标函数和梯度，导致即使在QUBO项具有有界因果锥的情况下，精确评估也面临可行性瓶颈。因此需要一种更高效的计算方法来降低资源消耗。

Method: 基于因果锥限制和伴随微分技术，LC-Implicit-QAOA首先分析锥结构和诱导边数，进行局部振幅和命名工作区的预分配；采用隐式设计（不存储全局状态和全局成本表），并在预算内联合选择等大小的微批次和检查点调度。

Result: 在p=2的有界锥网格上成功完成104个目标请求；在n≤24的验证上限下，对28个请求执行了参考计算，76个未运行；80个预算请求中内存使用始终在预算范围内（最高占用79.7%）；在3-正则n=512、p=2任务中，伴随法仅需101次目标等效调用和189秒，相比中心差分法的909次和1565秒大幅提升效率。

Conclusion: LC-Implicit-QAOA通过结构感知的局部化计算策略，在保证精度的同时显著降低了QAOA训练中的时间和内存开销，适用于固定深度的一阶和二阶局部对角QUBO问题，但不提供全局状态、采样或硬件无关的最优后端选择。

Abstract: QAOA training repeatedly queries an objective and all shared gradients, making exact evaluation a feasibility bottleneck even when QUBO terms have bounded causal cones. Building on established causal-cone restriction and adjoint differentiation, LC-Implicit-QAOA profiles cone structure and induced-edge counts before local-amplitude and named-workspace allocation, then jointly selects equal-size microbatches and checkpoint schedules under a named active-evaluator workspace budget. "Implicit" means omitting both global state and global cost table, not implicit differentiation; infeasible requests are rejected before those allocations. An independently implemented complex128/float64 dense adjoint agrees with LC over 1,800 graph-angle comparisons, with a worst relative gradient error of 1.56 x 10^-13. LC completes all 104 target requests in a p=2 bounded-cone grid; under a prespecified n <= 24 validation cap, the matched state-plus-cost reference is executed for 28 requests and deliberately not run on 76. Across 80 budgeted requests, measured allocated evaluator memory stays within budget, reaching at most 0.797 of it. On 3-regular n=512, p=2, the adjoint reaches the same finite-budget endpoint in 101 objective-equivalent calls and 189 s, versus 909 calls and 1,565 s for central differences. LC targets fixed-depth one- and two-local diagonal QUBO costs with a transverse-field mixer; it provides neither global states, sampling, nor a hardware-independent fastest-backend rule.

</details>


### [158] [RASP-QAOA: Resource-Aware Per-Instance Selection for Exact QAOA Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05646)
*Chih-Chung Hsu*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: RASP-QAOA是一种针对QAOA计算中不同表示方式的实例级选择器，能根据图结构、电路深度等特征智能选择最优计算路径，在多个测试中显著优于人工选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在QAOA仿真中，不同的计算表示方式在不同条件下表现差异大，但传统方法仅指定后端而忽略这些差异，导致效率低下。因此需要一种能自动适应实例特征的选择机制。

Method: 提出RASP-QAOA，首先过滤不满足语义或资源要求的动作，然后基于实例特征对剩余动作排序；对于超出训练分布的实例，使用分析性工作量估计进行处理。

Result: 在60个请求的评估中，RASP-QAOA在31个可执行请求中全部成功，top-1准确率为27/31，top-2为31/31，几何平均遗憾为1.051，PAR10分数仅为人工方法的0.0396倍；交叉验证显示图结构变化影响16个决策，且简单模型即可匹敌复杂模型。

Conclusion: 在n ≤ 35、p ≤ 5的范围内，基于资源感知的表示选择是有效的，性能提升主要来自表示本身的特征而非分类器复杂度。

Abstract: Exact QAOA simulation spans several computational representations whose useful regions differ sharply across graph structure, circuit depth, precision, and available memory. Choosing only a backend name hides these differences: an executable choice also fixes the representation, adapter, precision mode, and memory policy. We introduce RASP-QAOA, a per-instance selector over ten such actions. It first removes actions that cannot implement the requested QAOA semantics or execution requirements, then orders the remaining actions using instance features; actions outside learned support are handled by analytical work estimates. On a content-disjoint 60-request H200 evaluation, RASP-QAOA succeeds on all 31 requests for which at least one admissible action completes and validates. Within this set it reaches 27/31 top-1 and 31/31 top-2 selection, with 1.051 geometric-mean regret. Its failure-penalized PAR10 score is 0.0396 times that of development-selected CUAOA (95% interval: 0.0085-0.1644). A separate 30-request crossover shows that graph structure changes 16 decisions and improves the paired penalized score, while a depth-1 stump matches gradient boosting. The evidence supports resource-aware representation selection at n <= 35, p <= 5, with gains driven by representation features rather than classifier complexity.

</details>
