### [159] [Superconducting and charge-ordered phases from Dirac quantum spin liquids on the triangular lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05277)
*Andreas Feuerpfeil,Ronny Thomale,Subir Sachdev,Pietro M. Bonetti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一种从U(1)狄拉克自旋液体及其衍生态中产生超导和电荷有序相的通用理论框架，通过电荷为e的玻色子Higgs凝聚驱动相变，并构建了低能有效理论，揭示了包括d+id超导、电荷密度波等多种有序态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三角晶格量子自旋液体在压力或掺杂下如何转变为超导体，并解释光致增强太赫兹电导现象。

Method: 基于含有费米型自旋子的U(1)狄拉克自旋液体及其Z2和手性衍生态，构建低能有效理论，分析投影对称群对chargon对称性和色散关系的影响，并通过gauge不变的Higgs场复合物确定序参量。

Result: 发现了多种有序相，包括d+id超导、电荷密度波、键密度波和配对密度波；Higgs凝聚导致从自旋液体到常规超导和电荷有序相的转变。

Conclusion: 自旋液体到超导及电荷有序相的转变可通过spinless charge-e玻色chargon的Higgs机制统一描述，projective symmetry group决定了chargon性质，从而控制整个相图结构。

Abstract: Triangular-lattice quantum spin-liquid insulators are observed to undergo transitions to superconductivity under pressure or doping, and exhibit enhanced terahertz conductivity when driven by mid-infrared light. We present a general theoretical framework for the emergence of superconducting and charge-ordered phases from a U(1) Dirac spin liquid with fermionic spinons, as well as from its gapped $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and chiral descendants. Numerical studies have provided substantial evidence for these spin-liquid states.
  The spin-liquid phase hosts fractionalized Dirac spinons coupled to an emergent gauge field, whereas the superconducting and charge-ordered phases are conventional, with neither fractionalized excitations nor emergent gauge dynamics. The transition between these phases is driven by the Higgs condensation of spinless charge-$e$ bosonic chargons (``doublons'' and ``holons''). We show that the projective symmetry group of the Dirac spinons uniquely determines the symmetry and dispersion of the chargons, allowing us to construct an effective low-energy theory near the chargon band minima. Gauge-invariant composites of the chargon Higgs fields provide the order parameters characterizing the phases. The resulting phase diagram contains a rich variety of ordered states, including $d+id$ superconductivity, charge-density waves, bond-density waves, and pair-density waves.

</details>


### [160] [Neural Flux Attachment: From Bose Condensates to Chiral Topological Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06168)
*Rudik Badalyan,Khachatur G. Nazaryan,Tigran A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: ChernFormer是一种结合费米子变换器与固定陈-西蒙斯相位的神经网络波函数，能够统一描述玻色凝聚体和手性拓扑液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以使用单一神经波函数来描述既包含玻色凝聚又包含手性拓扑序的量子态。

Method: 提出ChernFormer模型，结合费米子变压器和固定陈-西蒙斯相位，使波函数在粒子交换下保持玻色对称性，并通过数值测试其在Kalmeyer-Laughlin基态及边缘态上的表现。

Result: ChernFormer在不同粒子数下均实现了接近 unity 的重叠积分，且在N=20时准确恢复了局部Laughlin涡旋和集体边缘涡旋结构；相比之下，传统连续对称波函数无法捕捉边缘激发模式。

Conclusion: ChernFormer提供了一个统一的变分框架，可用于同时描述常规玻色有序和手性拓扑物态。

Abstract: Can one neural wave function describe both a Bose condensate and a chiral topological liquid? We introduce ChernFormer, which combines a fermionic transformer with a fixed Chern-Simons phase that attaches one statistical vortex to every particle pair. Each factor changes sign under exchange, so their product is exactly bosonic. The fixed phase changes statistics but not probability, making every bosonic learning problem equivalent to a fermionic one with the same approximation error and overlap. With enough capacity, ChernFormer can approximate any normalizable bosonic wave function on the plane at fixed particle number. A finite, smooth network still vanishes when particles meet, yet this contact hole can shrink while the wave function and condensate fraction approach those of a nodeless condensate. Following the needle-in-a-haystack target-reconstruction benchmark introduced in \cite{NazaryanGaggioliTengFu2025}, we test ChernFormer on the Kalmeyer--Laughlin ground state and its first two chiral edge states. The overlap curves stay close to unity through their largest sampled sizes, while independent amplitude and phase maps at $N=20$ for all three states recover both local Laughlin vortices and the collective edge vortex. By contrast, a continuous, nonzero product of identical particle-wise factors misses these elementary edge sectors. ChernFormer therefore provides one variational language for conventional bosonic order and chiral topological matter.

</details>


### [161] [Self-dual $S_3$ gauge theory in 2+1d: lattice model and topological phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05294)
*Da-Chuan Lu,Chong Wang,Ashvin Vishwanath*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个非阿贝尔规范理论的电-磁自对偶格点模型，基于S₃量子双$\mathcal{D}(S_3)$，实现了任意子置换对称性，并发现其锯齿形边界支持无精细调节的临界边缘态。通过多种方法分析体相中的相变，提出最小凝聚原理，并推广至一系列二面体量子双模型，且均无符号问题，利于大规模数值研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找具有精确格点实现的非阿贝尔电-磁自对偶模型，以推广已知的Z₂规范理论自对偶结构，并探索非阿贝尔拓扑序中的对称性实现与相变机制。

Method: 构造基于S₃量子双$\mathcal{D}(S_3)$的格点哈密顿量，在张量积希尔伯特空间中通过半格点平移实现Z₂^{em}任意子置换对称性；结合范畴论任意子凝聚、微观格点模型和Chern-Simons-Higgs理论三种方法分析相变。

Result: 实现了S₃量子双中非阿贝尔电荷子C与涡旋子F的交换对称性；发现锯齿边界存在由四临界Ising共形场论描述的无能隙临界边缘态；体相存在三个保持Z₂^{em}对称性的玻色扰动，导致进入不同的拓扑有序相；所有方法一致支持最小凝聚原理；模型可推广至$\mathcal{D}(D_{2n})$族且无符号问题。

Conclusion: 成功构造了首个具有精确格点实现的非阿贝尔电-磁自对偶模型，揭示了任意子置换对称性的实现方式与相变中的普适行为，提出最小凝聚原理统一描述玻色任意子的凝聚过程，并为数值研究非阿贝尔Chern-Simons-Higgs理论提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Electric-magnetic self-duality of the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory, realized microscopically as a half-lattice-translation exchanging electric charge and magnetic flux, has been an influential example of a duality symmetry with an exact lattice realization. We construct the first non-Abelian generalization of this construction: a lattice model of the $S_3$ quantum double $\mathcal{D}(S_3)$ on a tensor product Hilbert space in which the $\mathbb{Z}^{\mathrm{em}}_2$ anyon-permutation symmetry, exchanging the non-Abelian chargeon $C$ and fluxon $F$, is realized via lattice translation. Consequently we find that the zigzag boundary termination of the model realizes, without fine-tuning, a gapless critical edge state described by the tetracritical Ising CFT. The bulk admits three independent $\mathbb{Z}_2^{\mathrm{em}}$-preserving bosonic perturbations, driving $\mathcal{D}(S_3)$ into distinct gapped phases. We analyze these transitions by three independent methods: category-theoretic anyon condensation, microscopic lattice Hamiltonians, and Chern-Simons-Higgs theory, which all agree, yielding a unified picture. These examples motivate a minimal-condensation principle: proliferating a bosonic anyon generically drives condensation of a minimal condensable algebra containing it, with symmetry-related condensates appearing as degenerate vacua that spontaneously break the anyon-permutation symmetry. Our model construction extends to an infinite family of self-dual dihedral quantum doubles $\mathcal{D}(D_{2n})$. Notably, each model is sign-problem-free, opening the door to large-scale numerical exploration of the phases of non-Abelian Chern-Simons-Higgs theories.

</details>


### [162] [Strongly Enhanced Charge-Density Waves and Correlated Insulating State in Atomically Thin 1$T$-TaS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05532)
*Gan Liu,Yulu Liu,Qiling Luo,Zhentao Huang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Meiyu Wang,Jinsheng Wen,Yi Lu,Xiaoxiang Xi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了1T-TaS2中厚度依赖的电荷密度波（CDW）相变，发现随着厚度减小，CDW增强，转变温度升高，单层中一级相变消失，源于库仑相互作用增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维极限下1T-TaS2中CDW相变的厚度依赖性及其物理机制。

Method: 采用温度依赖的拉曼光谱和电输运测量，并结合理论计算分析库仑屏蔽效应的影响。

Result: CDW相在单层极限下依然存在；厚度减小导致转变温度升高、面电阻显著增加、载流子局域长度锐减；单层中缺失一级CCDW-NCCDW相变；非局域屏蔽减弱导致库仑相互作用增强是主因。

Conclusion: 电子关联、电声耦合和层间耦合共同调控超薄1T-TaS2的基态和相变动力学，为调控二维CDW系统中的关联相提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate thickness-dependent charge-density-wave (CDW) transitions in 1$T$-TaS$_2$ using temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy and electrical transport. Raman measurements show that the incommensurate, nearly commensurate, and commensurate CDW phases persist down to the monolayer limit. As the thickness is reduced, the transition temperatures increase, accompanied by an orders-of-magnitude rise in sheet resistance and a sharp reduction in the carrier localization length. The first-order hysteretic CCDW-NCCDW transition is uniquely absent in the monolayer. Calculations suggest that the enhanced CDW in thin layers originates from strengthened Coulomb interactions due to reduced out-of-plane screening, particularly in the nonlocal component. These findings highlight the cooperative roles of electron correlation, electron-phonon interaction, and interlayer coupling in shaping the ground state and transition dynamics of atomically thin 1$T$-TaS$_2$, opening pathways for engineering correlated phases in two-dimensional CDW systems.

</details>


### [163] [Local Spin Excitations Mediate Quasiparticle Breakdown in the Orbital-Selective Mott Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05556)
*Yuekun Niu,Yu Ni,Jia-Ming Wang,Zhong-Yi Lu,Yun Song,Shiping Feng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过动力学平均场理论揭示了在轨道选择性Mott相中，由于洪德耦合产生的局域自旋激发导致不同轨道间电子耦合，破坏准粒子相干性，从而解释了准粒子崩塌的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索轨道选择性Mott相中准粒子崩塌现象背后的未知微观起源。

Method: 采用两带Hubbard模型的动力学平均场理论，分析洪德耦合对局域自旋激发及电子行为的影响。

Result: 发现自旋翻转和Ising型洪德耦合会引发局域自旋激发，这些激发耦合不同轨道中的电子，改变准粒子寿命和结合能，导致准粒子失去明确定义；移除这些耦合则恢复准粒子相干性和电荷动力学的解耦。

Conclusion: 电子与局域自旋激发之间的耦合是驱动轨道选择性Mott相中准粒子崩塌的根本机制。

Abstract: The orbital-selective Mott phase (OSMP) is commonly described as a coexistence of localized and itinerant electrons within effectively decoupled orbitals, but emerging evidence for quasiparticle breakdown points to physics beyond this picture, whose microscopic origin remains unknown. Using dynamical mean-field theory for the two-band Hubbard model, we show that the spin-flip and Ising-type components of Hund's coupling generate local spin excitations (LSEs). These LSEs couple electrons between different orbitals, renormalize quasiparticle lifetimes and binding energies, and thereby destroy well-defined quasiparticles in the OSMP. Removing these two components of Hund's coupling restores coherent quasiparticle behavior and fully decouples the charge dynamics of the two bands. Our results therefore identify electronic coupling to LSEs as the fundamental mechanism driving quasiparticle breakdown within the OSMP.

</details>


### [164] [Helical-to-Fan Transitions under Magnetic Fields in the Noncentrosymmetric Tetragonal Magnet EuRhGe$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05709)
*Takeshi Matsumura,Hiroto Nitta,Hironori Nakao,Masashi Kakihana,Masato Hedo,Takao Nakama,Yoshichika Ōnuki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: EuRhGe$_3$表现出从圆螺旋磁序到自旋翻转$xyz$-扇形（椭圆锥形）再到平面$xy$-扇形结构的完整外场诱导相变序列，是理论预言的典型实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非中心对称磁性材料EuRhGe$_3$在外加磁场下的磁结构演化，验证理论预测的螺旋磁体场致相变序列。

Method: 采用共振X射线衍射技术研究EuRhGe$_3$在不同磁场条件下的磁结构变化。

Result: 发现EuRhGe$_3$在低温下呈现非公度螺旋磁序，并随磁场增强依次经历二次谐波峰出现、锁相转变、螺旋性消失进入$xyz$-扇形态，最终转变为无$c$轴分量的平面$xy$-扇形态。

Conclusion: EuRhGe$_3$是展现从圆螺旋到$xyz$-扇形再到$xy$-扇形完整场致相变过程的典型螺旋磁体，实验结果与理论预测一致。

Abstract: The magnetic structure of EuRhGe$_3$, a noncentrosymmetric body-centered tetragonal magnet with the space group $I4mm$, has been investigated by resonant X-ray diffraction. Below $T_{\text{N}}=12$ K, EuRhGe$_3$ undergoes a helical magnetic ordering with an incommensurate propagation vector $q=(0, 0, 0.809)$, in which the magnetic moments lie in the $ab$ plane and rotate by a constant turn angle of $145.8^{\circ}$ between adjacent layers. When a magnetic field is applied along the $a$ axis at 2 K, a second-harmonic $2q$ peak develops, indicating that the circular helix is gradually distorted into a helimagnetic soliton-lattice state, which eventually undergoes a lock-in transition to the commensurate structure with $q=0.8$ at 3.8 T. Above the subsequent phase boundary at 5 T, the helicity is lost, and a spin-flop $xyz$-fan (elliptic conical) state is realized, in which the moments oscillate predominantly along the $b$ axis but are accompanied by a small $c$-axis component. At higher fields, the system enters a conventional planar $xy$-fan phase without a $c$-axis component. EuRhGe$_3$ provides a prototypical example of a helimagnet that exhibits a full sequence of field-induced structures, evolving from a circular helix to a spin-flop $xyz$-fan (elliptic conical), and finally to a planar $xy$-fan structure, which has been theoretically predicted.

</details>


### [165] [Correlated topological-polarization surface states in the narrow-gap insulator FeSb2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05887)
*Takahiro Iwagaki,Hideki Matsuoka,Ginta Hoshino,Kanata Watanabe,Shungo Aoyagi,Shunsuke Kitou,Yuiga Nakamura,Motoaki Hirayama,Takashi Koretsune,Naoya Kanazawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究展示了拓扑极化作为一种设计原理，能够在强关联的3d过渡金属化合物中实现拓扑表面态，并在FeSb2薄膜中观测到由体相强关联驱动的非互易表面输运和可栅控的量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子关联与能带拓扑通常因元素需求冲突而难以共存，研究旨在通过拓扑极化机制将二者结合，拓展关联电子体系中的拓扑物态设计空间。

Method: 利用外延生长的FeSb2窄带隙绝缘体薄膜，结合输运测量与电场调控手段，研究其表面态的拓扑-极化起源及其与体相3d轨道重构的关系。

Result: 发现FeSb2薄膜具有源于拓扑极化的金属表面态；在体相强关联导致的Fe 3d轨道重构温度以下出现非互易表面输运，证实了关联体系中的体-边对应；并通过静电门控使该关联表面发生量子相变，进入铁磁或可能的交替磁态。

Conclusion: 拓扑极化为在广泛材料（尤其是强关联3d过渡金属化合物）中实现和调控拓扑量子相提供了新路径。

Abstract: Strong electron correlations and band topology each generate rich quantum phases, but conflicting elemental requirements have largely kept them apart. Topological polarization offers a route to unite them, producing polar surface states from bonding charge without spin-orbit coupling and thereby extending band topology to correlated 3d transition-metal compounds. Here we demonstrate that epitaxial thin films of the narrow-gap insulator FeSb2 host metallic polar surface states of topological-polarization origin, governed by the strong correlations of the bulk. Nonreciprocal surface transport emerges only below the onset temperature of a correlation-driven reconstruction of the bulk Fe 3d orbital occupation, providing direct evidence of bulk-edge correspondence in a correlated topological system. Moreover, electrostatic gating drives this correlated surface across a quantum phase transition into a ferromagnetic or possibly altermagnetic state. Our results establish topological polarization as a design principle for correlated topological phases in a broad range of materials.

</details>


### [166] [Landscape of incompressible crystals of hard-core bosons on the square-kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05927)
*Pratyay Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了平方-笼目晶格上的硬核玻色子模型，使用超越传统单胞描述的分层平均场理论，揭示了更多不可压缩态，并预测了实验相关材料中的额外磁化平台结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单胞描述无法完全捕捉平方-笼目晶格支持的不可压缩态的层次结构，因此需要更广泛的变分簇方法来揭示更多量子相。

Method: 采用基于扩大变分簇的分层平均场理论，并结合Matsubara-Matsuda映射，研究硬核玻色子模型及其对应的自旋-1/2 XXZ模型。

Result: 重现了密度为5/6和2/3的已知紧致局域态相，并发现新的不可压缩态，特别是密度为3/4的稳健相；该相对应于XXZ模型中的半磁化平台；在两个实际材料中计算的磁化过程与实验吻合良好，并预测了新的平台结构。

Conclusion: 扩展的平均场方法能更全面地揭示平方-笼目晶格的量子相图，且密度为3/4的相具有强鲁棒性，为实验观测提供了新预测。

Abstract: We investigate a hard-core boson model on the square-kagome lattice using hierarchical mean-field theory beyond the conventional unit-cell description. We show that the conventional description of the square-kagome lattice based on its smallest unit cell does not fully capture the hierarchy of incompressible states supported by the lattice, but only captures the two compact-localized-state-based phases at densities $5/6$ and $2/3$. Within our approach, we reproduce these previously established phases and uncover additional incompressible states enabled by enlarging the variational cluster. Among these, the $ρ=3/4$ phase is found to be particularly robust, which, through the Matsubara-Matsuda mapping, corresponds to a half-magnetization plateau in the spin-$1/2$ XXZ model. We further apply our approach to two experimentally relevant square-kagome compounds using exchange parameters obtained from first-principles calculations. The calculated magnetization processes are in good agreement with available experimental results and predict additional plateau structures in these materials.

</details>


### [167] [Dissipation-induced bulk and boundary criticality in the Haldane chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05984)
*Zhenjiu Wang,Manuel Weber*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了哈达玛链与耗散欧姆浴耦合下的量子相变，发现其存在由相互作用不动点主导的二级量子相变，并揭示了拓扑边缘模式在临界性中的独特行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索耗散环境下对称保护拓扑态的稳定性及其量子相变行为，特别是边界临界性的调控机制。

Method: 采用大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟，并结合耗散φ^4理论的ε展开方法进行分析。

Result: 发现动态指数z≈2的相互作用固定点主导的二级相变；提出广义弦序参量，显示弱耗散下拓扑态稳定且在临界点具有非平凡标度维度；拓扑边缘模式表现出区别于平凡态的独特边界临界性。

Conclusion: 哈达玛链的拓扑态在弱耗散下稳定，耗散可连续调节边界临界性，为实现可调谐的边界临界现象提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study the Haldane chain coupled to a dissipative ohmic bath. Using large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we identify a second-order quantum phase transition into an antiferromagnetic state with spontaneously broken SO(3) symmetry that is governed by an interacting fixed point with dynamical exponent $z\approx 2$. We derive a generalized string order parameter which indicates that the symmetry-protected topological ground state of the Haldane chain is stable for weak dissipation and develops a nontrivial scaling dimension at criticality. In particular, its topological edge modes display nontrivial boundary criticality that is distinct from an equivalent transition out of a trivial state; for the latter, we perform an accurate $ε$ expansion of a dissipative $φ^4$ theory with ordinary boundary conditions. Our setup realizes a spin impurity in a critical yet nonconformal antiferromagnet and paves the way for continuously tunable boundary criticality controlled by dissipation.

</details>


### [168] [Resilient strange metal at an unconventional quantum critical point in $d=2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05988)
*Jérôme Leblanc,A. -M. S. Tremblay*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维Hubbard模型在量子临界点附近的奇异金属行为，利用非微扰的改进双粒子自洽方法预测了Kohn异常导致的非常规临界指数，并发现电阻率随温度线性变化，尽管费米面上的自能没有明显的准粒子特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低于Mott转变强度的相互作用下，费米液体与非共线自旋密度波序之间的量子临界点处的奇异金属行为及其与量子临界性质的关系。

Method: 采用非微扰的改进双粒子自洽方法，分析关联长度在约一个到一百个晶格间距范围内的系统特性。

Result: 发现Kohn异常导致费米面上出现非常规临界指数，单粒子自能在费米面上表现出强烈的动量依赖性，缺乏Landau准粒子证据，但依然观察到电阻率随温度线性变化的奇异金属行为。

Conclusion: 电阻率的线性温度依赖性可以在没有费米面上线性自能的情况下产生，挑战了通常假设的线性自能导致线性电阻的观点。

Abstract: Properties of the two-dimensional Hubbard model with nearest-neighbor hopping are under scrutiny in cold-atom experiments and diagrammatic quantum Monte Carlo. Given the controlled nature of these approaches, it is timely to make predictions about strange-metal behavior and its relation to quantum-critical properties. Even for interaction strengths below the Mott transition, several peculiarities occur at the quantum critical point separating Fermi liquid and incommensurate spin-density wave order. Here, we predict, using the non-perturbative improved two-particle self-consistent approach, that for correlation lengths ranging from about one to one hundred lattice spacings, Kohn anomalies on the underlying Fermi surface lead to unconventional critical exponents. The temperature dependence of the single-particle self-energy acquires strong momentum dependence along the Fermi surface with no clear evidence of Landau quasiparticles. Nevertheless, we observe strange-metal behavior, namely, resistivity that scales linearly with temperature as $T\rightarrow{0}$. Our work shows that a linear temperature dependence of resistivity arises without a linear-in-temperature self-energy along the Fermi surface, as is often assumed.

</details>


### [169] [Phonon spectral functions of low-density polaron metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06357)
*Luis Walther,Alberto Nocera,Mona Berciu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文使用密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）方法计算了一维无自旋Holstein模型在低载流子浓度下的声子谱函数，并与RPA及改进方案进行比较，揭示了非微扰效应下声子谱权重的显著转移现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究低但有限载流子浓度下一维Holstein模型的声子谱函数，填补单极化子极限（x→0）之外的研究空白，并检验传统Kohn异常和Migdal近似在该区域的适用性。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）方法精确计算电子-声子耦合系统中的声子谱函数；同时结合随机相位近似（RPA）、‘ dressed RPA’（结合动量平均MA近似）以及包含顶点修正的方法进行对比分析。

Result: 发现声子谱权重显著转移到零频率以下和裸声子能量Ω以上，未观测到对应2k_F的Kohn反常行为；dressed RPA能半定量复现DMRG结果，而加入最低阶顶点修正会导致非物理的负谱权重。

Conclusion: 在低密度极化子金属体系中，传统的Migdal-Gorkov微扰框架失效，必须一致地处理传播子和顶点修正；dressed RPA是一种高效且可靠的近似方法，适用于弱掺杂绝缘体等系统。

Abstract: We use the density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) to compute the phonon spectral function of a one-dimensional spinless Holstein model doped with a low but finite carrier concentration, $x \leq 0.15$, as a function of the electron-phonon coupling $λ$. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first such results in this regime, complementing extensive prior work at the single-polaron level ($x\to 0$). We find that significant phonon spectral weight is transferred both below, all the way down to $ω=0$, and above the bare phonon energy $Ω$, in stark contrast with the Kohn-anomaly phenomenology expected in the Migdal limit, where weight remains centered near $Ω$ with a kink at $q=2k_F$. No signature of this $2k_F$ kink appears in our results. This behavior is captured qualitatively by the Random Phase Approximation (RPA), and semi-quantitatively, at negligible extra computational cost, by a ``dressed RPA'' scheme in which the electron addition propagator is renormalized using the Momentum Average (MA) approximation for the low-density electron-polaron. By contrast, adding the lowest-order vertex correction to this dressed scheme produces unphysical negative spectral weight, signaling that vertex and propagator dressings must be treated consistently once the propagators are dressed nonperturbatively. Our results provide an efficient approximation for the phonon spectral functions of low-density polaron metals, a regime relevant to weakly doped insulators.

</details>

### [170] [Entangling power of neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05293)
*Taige Wang,Nisarga Paul,Liang Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于编码器-解码器神经网络的纠缠能力度量方法，用于量化子系统间的纠缠生成能力，并揭示了其在适度资源下的指数级纠缠能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解和量化机器学习模型中子系统之间的复杂关联，受量子纠缠理论启发，引入新的度量方式。

Method: 定义了神经网络的纠缠能力，针对K维潜在空间和多项式解码器（次数p）精确计算该度量。

Result: 证明了具有多项式解码器的神经网络在适度潜在空间维度下可实现指数级纠缠能力。

Conclusion: 所提框架推广了纠缠理论中的Schmidt秩概念，为分析机器学习中的相关性提供了新工具。

Abstract: Characterizing the complexity of correlations between subsystems is a fundamental task across information theory, machine learning, and science. In quantum physics, neural networks have found increasing application in learning wavefunctions. Here we introduce the entangling power of an encoder-decoder neural network, which quantifies its ability to generate entanglement between subsystems, dependent on a latent space dimension $K$ and the complexity class of the decoder. We exactly calculate this quantity for polynomial decoders of degree $p$ acting on a $K$-dimensional latent space. Our results establish the exponential entangling power of neural networks with modest resources. More broadly, our work provides a framework for analyzing correlations in machine learning that generalizes the notion of the Schmidt rank in entanglement theory.

</details>


<div id='physics.geo-ph'></div>

# physics.geo-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Marchenko Theory, Algorithms, and Applications: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05214)
*Hammed A. Oyekan*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了Marchenko方法在地震数据处理中的应用，该方法利用地表单侧测量数据重建地下格林函数，仅需平滑的速度模型即可估计直达波走时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地震成像将多次波视为噪声，而Marchenko方法能够利用这些信息，提升地下结构成像精度。

Method: 基于一维量子力学逆散射理论发展而来，通过声学、弹性及电磁互易定理推广至多维介质，并结合地震干涉、波场聚焦和全波形反演等技术。

Result: 实现了内部多次波消除、目标导向处理、红数据化成像等功能，在海洋、陆地、盐下和时移监测等实际数据中取得良好效果。

Conclusion: Marchenko方法为地震成像提供了新范式，但仍面临弹性多分量扩展、联合反演和神经网络加速等挑战。

Abstract: Seismic reflection data collected at the Earth's surface carry information from every depth in the subsurface, including the internal multiples that conventional migration treats as noise or artefacts. The Marchenko method retrieves subsurface Green's functions directly from these single-sided measurements. The only model information it requires is a smooth macro-velocity model, used to estimate the direct-wave traveltime from the surface to a virtual point at depth. The method originates in one-dimensional quantum mechanical inverse scattering and has been generalised over the past two decades to three-dimensional acoustic media using acoustic reciprocity theorems, and to elastic and electromagnetic media using the corresponding elastodynamic and Maxwell reciprocity theorems. This review covers the classical Gelfand--Levitan--Marchenko equation and its derivation through the Schrodinger scattering formalism, the seismic interferometry results that motivated the multidimensional extension, and the coupled Marchenko equations for wave focusing in higher dimensions. On the application side it treats Marchenko redatuming, single- and double-sided imaging conditions, internal multiple elimination, target-oriented processing, and full-waveform inversion. Extensions to dissipative media, elastodynamics, electromagnetic fields, and plane-wave acquisition are also covered, along with practical topics such as least-squares implementations, GPU acceleration, compressive-sensing acquisition, and recent machine-learning approaches to predicting the focusing functions. Field-data results from marine, land, sub-salt, and time-lapse monitoring settings are summarised. The review closes with an assessment of current limitations and open problems, among them elastic multi-component extensions, joint inversion, and neural-network accelerators.

</details>


### [172] [Foundation Model-Assisted Full Waveform Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05763)
*Mustafa Alfarhan,Matteo Ravasi,Fuqiang Chen,George Turkiyyah,David Keyes*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预训练地震基础模型SeisLM的全波形反演（FWI）新目标函数，利用冻结编码器提取的特征差异构建反演梯度，有效缓解了传统L2范数目标函数在初始模型不佳时易陷入周期跳变的问题。实验表明，该方法在Marmousi、2D和3D Overthrust模型中均能更稳健地引导反演收敛，尤其在复杂场景下显著优于传统及混合目标函数，验证了预训练模型特征用于早期FWI目标设计的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全波形反演（FWI）依赖波形差的目标函数容易因初始模型不准确而出现周期跳跃问题，导致反演失败。为解决这一挑战，本文旨在探索利用预训练地震基础模型SeisLM提取的深层特征来构建更具鲁棒性的目标函数，以增强反演对初始模型误差的容忍度。

Method: 提出一种新的FWI目标函数，使用预训练且参数冻结的SeisLM模型编码器从模拟和观测地震道中提取特征，并计算特征差异作为目标函数；通过链式法则将特征差异的梯度反向传播至模拟数据，生成适用于标准伴随态框架的伴随源；同时测试了一种结合SeisLM特征损失与传统L2目标的调度混合损失策略。

Result: 时间偏移诊断显示，SeisLM特征损失的目标函数具有更宽更平滑的吸引盆；在Marmousi、2D和3D Overthrust实验中，SeisLM特征损失在初期反演中表现优异，尤其在3D Overthrust中成功引导出可进一步精炼的背景模型，而传统L2反演则停滞不前；过早引入L2项可能重新引发周期跳跃。

Conclusion: 使用预训练地震网络提取的特征构建早期FWI目标函数是有效的，相比完全替代波形域误差点，将其用于引导初始反演过程更为合理，能够显著提升反演的稳健性和收敛性。

Abstract: Full waveform inversion (FWI) can recover high-resolution subsurface velocity models. Conventional waveform-difference objectives, however, are vulnerable to cycle skipping when the starting model is inaccurate. We introduce an FWI objective that compares features produced from modeled and observed seismic traces by SeisLM, a pretrained seismic foundation model. The SeisLM encoder remains frozen during inversion, and the feature discrepancy is differentiated with respect to the modeled traces to construct an adjoint source compatible with the standard adjoint-state framework. We also test a scheduled hybrid loss that combines the SeisLM feature loss with the conventional $L_2$ objective. Time-shift diagnostics show that the loss computed from features produced by the pretrained encoder has a broader and smoother basin around the correct alignment than either the waveform $L_2$ objective or the feature loss obtained from an encoder with the same architecture and randomly initialized parameters. In the Marmousi experiment, the SeisLM and hybrid objectives produce similar improvements during early-stage inversion and provide useful models for subsequent reflection-based $L_2$ refinement. In the 2D Overthrust experiment, which begins from a laterally invariant linear-gradient model, the SeisLM feature-loss workflow outperforms the conventional and hybrid workflows, indicating that introducing the $L_2$ contribution too early can reintroduce cycle-skipping sensitivity. In the 3D Overthrust experiment, conventional $L_2$ inversion stalls near the initial linear gradient, whereas the SeisLM feature loss guides the inversion toward a background model from which $L_2$ refinement recovers the principal structures. These results support using features produced by pretrained seismic networks to define early-stage FWI objectives rather than complete replacements for waveform-domain misfits.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Likelihood-based anomaly detection in preferential attachment networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05182)
*Qiu Liang,Remco van der Hofstad,Nelly Litvak*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在优先连接（PA）网络中出现异常节点的检测问题，提出了一种基于似然的检测框架和迭代参数估计方法，能够较准确地估计模型参数，且在中期出现的异常检测效果最好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于PA网络中节点度增长的高度变异性以及后期异常出现时信息有限，检测此类异常具有挑战性，因此需要有效的检测方法。

Method: 提出一种迭代参数估计方法，并结合基于似然的检测框架来识别具有附加连接优势的异常节点。

Result: 模拟结果显示该方法能准确估计参数β和δ；检测性能依赖于异常出现的时间，中期出现的异常检测最可靠，而早期和晚期异常尤其是当β较小时仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 所提出的检测框架在PA网络中对中期出现的异常节点具有良好的检测能力，但在极端时间点（过早或过晚）及小β值情况下仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Preferential attachment (PA) network is a widely used model for capturing the growth dynamics of real-world networks, in which newly arriving vertices are more likely to connect to existing vertices with higher degrees. In this paper, we consider a setting in which an anomalous vertex appears at some time point and receives edges with an additional attachment advantage governed by a parameter $β$, while ordinary vertices continue to follow the PA mechanism with parameter $δ$. Detecting such anomalies is challenging due to the high variability in degree growth in PA networks and the limited information available when the anomaly arrives late in the network evolution. We propose an iterative parameter estimation procedure together with a likelihood-based detection framework. Simulation results show that the proposed procedure provides accurate estimation of the parameters $β$ and $δ$. Detection performance depends on the time of anomaly occurrence: anomalies arising at midway stages of the network evolution are detected most reliably, whereas very early and late anomalies remain challenging, particularly when the parameter $β$ is small.

</details>


### [174] [Automatic Detection of Deaths from Social Networking Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05183)
*Nuhu Ibrahim,Riza Batista-Navarro*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本论文研究了社交媒体上用户去世前后发布内容的语言特征差异，并开发了高性能的机器学习模型来自动检测用户的死亡事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在识别社交网络中与用户死亡相关的文本特征，实现对用户死亡的自动检测，为社交媒体分析和数字遗产管理提供技术支持。

Method: 构建了一个结合Wikidata和Twitter的新数据集，使用TF-IDF和预训练词嵌入（Glove、Word2Vec、FastText）提取特征，训练传统机器学习模型（RF、KNN、LR、SVM）和深度学习模型（BiLSTM、CNN、BERT）进行分类。

Result: 随机森林（RF）在传统模型中表现最佳；BiLSTM优于CNN；TF-IDF在传统模型中优于词嵌入；Word2Vec在深度学习模型中优于Glove和FastText；BERT整体性能最优。发现逝后推文负面情绪更高，包含更多死亡、宗教、家庭相关词汇及人称代词和动词，而生前推文更中性且含更多非正式用语。

Conclusion: 成功开发出一种高性能的自动检测社交网络用户死亡的技术，揭示了生死相关文本在情感和语言使用上的系统性差异，具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: This dissertation analysed and discussed the differences in linguistic characteristics between pre-mortem and post-mortem social media content, and reported machine learning (ML) classifiers that achieved high performance in automatically detecting deaths of social networking site users from posts associated with their profiles. A new dataset was developed using Wikidata and Twitter. ML models, both traditional (RF, KNN, LR, and SVM) and deep learning (BiLSTM, CNN, and the state-of-the-art BERT), were trained on features extracted using TF-IDF and pre-trained embeddings (Glove, Word2Vec, and FastText) to classify post-mortem content from its pre-mortem counterpart. The results showed that RF outperformed all other traditional ML models; BiLSTM outperformed CNN; TF-IDF consistently outperformed pre-trained word embeddings for the traditional models; Word2Vec consistently outperformed Glove and FastText for the deep learning models; and BERT outperformed all other models. It was found that although pre-mortem and post-mortem tweets express similar levels of positive sentiment, post-mortem tweets exhibit higher negative sentiment, whereas pre-mortem tweets exhibit higher neutral sentiment. Feelings suggesting negativity (sad, angry, surprise, and fear) are more dominant in post-mortem tweets, while happy is more dominant in pre-mortem tweets. It was also found that words, personal pronouns, verbs, family words, religious words, death words, and swear words occur more frequently in post-mortem tweets, whereas impersonal pronouns and informal words occur more frequently in pre-mortem tweets. Additionally, analytical thinking is expressed more in post-mortem than pre-mortem conversations. This experiment's significant contribution is the successful development of an exceptionally high-performing technique for automatically detecting user deaths on social networking sites.

</details>


### [175] [IMMENSE: Inductive Multi-perspective User Classification in Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05259)
*Francesco Benedetti,Antonio Pellicani,Gianvito Pio,Michelangelo Ceci*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为IMMENSE的机器学习方法，用于检测社交媒体中的恶意用户，结合内容语义、社交关系和空间信息进行混合分类，并采用归纳学习以适应新用户和网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于在线社交网络中存在大量传播歧视、仇恨和暴力内容的用户，尤其是对年轻用户造成心理和社会危害，因此需要有效系统帮助执法机构识别此类恶意用户。

Method: IMMENSE采用基于机器学习的混合分类策略，整合用户发布内容的语义、社交关系和空间信息，并使用归纳学习方法，无需重新训练即可对新用户或新网络进行分类。

Result: 在真实Twitter/X数据集上的实验表明，IMMENSE优于五种最先进的基线方法，验证了其在社交网络监控系统中部署的有效性。

Conclusion: IMMENSE通过融合多视角上下文信息和归纳学习机制，在检测恶意用户方面表现出优越性能，适合大规模动态社交网络的实际应用。

Abstract: Online social networks increasingly expose people to users who propagate discriminatory, hateful, and violent content. Young users, in particular, are vulnerable to exposure to such content, which can have harmful psychological and social repercussions. Given the massive scale of today's social networks, in terms of both published content and number of users, there is an urgent need for effective systems to aid Law Enforcement Agencies (LEAs) in identifying and addressing users that disseminate malicious content. In this work we introduce IMMENSE, a machine learning-based method for detecting malicious social network users. Our approach adopts a hybrid classification strategy that integrates three perspectives: the semantics of the users' published content, their social relationships and their spatial information. Such contextual perspectives potentially enhance classification performance beyond text-only analysis. Importantly, IMMENSE employs an inductive learning approach, enabling it to classify previously unseen users or entire new networks without the need for costly and time-consuming model retraining procedures. Experiments carried out on a real-world Twitter/X dataset showed the superiority of IMMENSE against five state of the art competitors, confirming the benefits of its hybrid approach for effective deployment in social network monitoring systems.

</details>


### [176] [SnapScope: A Platform for City-Scale Collection and Exploration of Public Snap Map Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05841)
*Mohammed Almukaynizi,Fahad Alhaqbani,Khaled Almarzoug,Sultan Alanbari*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为SnapScope的集成平台，用于在城市规模上收集和管理Snapchat Snap Map上的公开地理标记视频和图片数据，并在沙特阿拉伯利雅得部署应用，展示了其高效的数据采集与探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于Snapchat未提供API且缺乏可重现的大规模数据收集方法，研究旨在开发一个可重复、可交互的系统来收集和探索Snap Map上的公开数据。

Method: 设计并实现SnapScope平台，包含后端采集流水线和基于Web的前端界面，支持采集管理、交互式数据探索、区域对比和数据导出，在利雅得1公里网格的2740个查询点上持续23天进行数据采集。

Result: 共收集515,364条唯一公开快照，连续21次饱和探测显示94.8%的结果为重复记录，验证了数据采集的稳定性与效率。

Conclusion: SnapScope是一个可复用、城市无关的数据采集平台，能够有效支持Snap Map类数据的城市级研究，提供的聚合数据集符合隐私保护要求，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Snapchat's Snap Map is an ephemeral stream of geotagged public video and image stories, but the platform provides no documented API, no prior work describes a reproducible system for collecting this data at city scale, and no tool exists for managing and exploring the collected data interactively. We present SnapScope, an integrated platform that pairs a back-end collection pipeline with a web-based front end for scraper management, interactive data exploration, side-by-side neighborhood comparison, and data export. We deploy the platform over Riyadh, Saudi Arabia, collecting 515,364 unique public snaps across 23 days on a 1 km grid of 2,740 query points. A saturation probe over 21 consecutive runs shows that 94.8% of returned observations are duplicates of already-stored records. We provide a privacy-safe aggregate dataset under CC BY 4.0. The platform is city-agnostic and redeployable by substituting grid coordinates and boundary polygons.

</details>
